The reason they put forward to the banks for the refund is usually false and now the merchant has lost out due to this fraud. This can cause a major loss in income if it happens enough. If you are a business owner, you might have experienced this at some point. Businesses often just take this as the cost of doing business as there is not much they can do from their side. Often opening up an investigation and going through the process would be more time consuming and costly than just counting it as a loss.
With cryptocurrencies that use blockchains, transactions are irreversible. If someone pays you an amount, there is no way that they can get it back, unless you send it back to them. This type of protection has been built into the blockchain system to prevent the merchant from fraud and false payments. As a business owner, you would have to stipulate your returns policy and then stick to it. It is also the responsibility of the merchant to refund the customer should there be a real reason for the refund.
Faster Transaction Speeds
If you were to make an international transaction from the USA to anywhere else in the world, it would definitely take some time for the recipient to get the money. This is because there are various steps that have been put in place by banks in the approval process. The money would have to go through the USA banking systems and then go through the other countries banking systems. This does not only affect the fees associated with the transaction, but the speed of the transaction.
With cryptocurrency there are no central banks that the money has to go through and be approved by. As soon as you make the transaction, it is approved by the system. Think of it as the difference between sending a letter in the mail versus sending an email. If you send the letter through the mail, the person will only get it in a few days’ time because it has to go through your post office and then reach the post office of the person you have sent it to. With an email, it can be sent immediately because it is just sent directly from person to person using the internet.
It should be noted that recently Bitcoin transactions have been slower to get approved and sent to the recipient. This is because the amount of Bitcoin users that are performing transactions has increased by quite a bit. The more people that are using the platform, the slower it will be to have the transactions approved. However, even with this slow down, it is still much faster than a regular bank transaction. Before the influx of new users the transaction speed would not be more than a few minutes. Now it is looking like a few hours, but that depends on how many people are transferring their Bitcoin at that moment. If you put through a transaction at a time with low activity your transaction will go through faster.
There Is No Risk of Inflation
Inflation is more of a problem in third world countries. With the struggling economies of these developing countries, their governments often have to inflate prices in order to keep their head above water. They have to make more money by printing more banknotes and adding more money to their economy, which results in the value of the individual not going down. This puts the people that live in these countries at a major disadvantage. Inflation is often not announced in advance and the people have to deal with the repercussions of the increased price of items while their salaries stay the same. This also impacts savings. If someone in a developing country has $10,000 of their currency and the inflation rate goes up by 20%, this means that they have essentially lost 20% of their savings because they can buy 20% less than what they could have before the inflation took place. This would mean that they would have the equivalent of $8,000 instead of the $10,000 that is actually reflected in their bank account.
Usually the inflation rates don’t go up by that much at one time, but it is definitely possible. There have been cases of hyperinflation where inflation rates would go up by over 100% and create hyperinflation that is almost an uncontrollable increase in prices for everything. This can decimate the savings of people in these countries. Essentially, they will have no money to live off because their salaries would not be able to pay for it. This makes it even harder for people in these countries to climb out of poverty or even save up enough money to move to another country that has a more stable economy.
The United States and other first world countries also have inflation, granted not on such an extreme scale. Some have stated that the inflation rates are higher than what is stated in the Consumer Price Index. The problem with inflation is that the regular citizen has no control over it. If the government makes bad decisions that cause a negative impact on the economy, all the citizens will suffer for it out of no fault of their own (Brock, 2017).
With Bitcoin and other cryptocurrencies, there is no risk of inflation because the worth of the is not dictated by a person or an entity. There are specific algorithms and systems in place that allow it to function without the risk of inflation. One of the biggest factors in this, with regards to Bitcoin, is that there is only a certain amount of Bitcoin in the world. Once all of it is in circulation more of it cannot be made. This means that the value of the cryptocurrency will not be defined by the amount of it in circulation; it is fixed and everything will have to work according to that. This is the same principle as the Gold Standard, which is how money used to be handled in the 1800s and the early 1900s.
Your Information Is Not Stored on the System or That of the Merchants
When you shop online, your card details are often stored on the system. You have to put in your credit card details into the system so that it can process the transaction. There is a level of uncertainty when it comes to this, because you can never be sure what will happen with your details after this. Your card number is tied to your name and other personal information. Even if the merchant doesn’t do anything with that information, there is always a risk of you getting hacked. If a cybercriminal is able to hack into the merchants’ database, they can steal all the card numbers and other personal details of the customers. If you think this is not a possibility, it has already happened to the likes of Home Depot and Target. These are huge stores, with a massive customer base. The amount of information that the hackers would have been able to get ahold of would have been quite substantial.
With cryptocurrency, everything happens without the use of personal information. You don’t give out a card number or any other personal details. Instead you use a temporary encrypted code that cannot actually be traced back to you. This is great for both the merchant and the customer as it increases the level of trust between the two. People will be more willing to transact with a merchant if they know that there is no risk of their information getting out or the system being hacked. The merchant will also not have the added responsibility of making sure that any personal information the customer gives them is kept safe. The merchant will not have to deal with hackers, which can be a costly process.
Ease of International Transactions
Many of us have done international transactions before. Whether it be to send money to a friend or family member in another country or to buy something internationally. It is much easier to make these transactions when you don’t have to incur large bank fees or have to wait long periods of time for the money to be cleared. If you are purchasing something from overseas you will be able to receive it quicker because the transaction would be cleared in a short amount of time.
If you are a business owner, this means that you will be able to expand your business to international customers and clients. People would be more likely to buy from you if they will not incur large fees for sending through the money. Your business will also not be subject to economic fluctuations in those other countries. You can set a standard price across the board and not have to deal with the complications of a weaker economy of a country you are doing business with.

Chapter 2:
What Is Decentralized Finance and Does It Have a Place in Today’s World?
Throughout this book, you will be hearing a lot about decentralized finance. It is important to understand what exactly this means in order to understand the significance it plays in today’s world. There is a lot to be said about it and there are clear benefits, as we can see by the rise in cryptocurrency. Bitcoin may be the largest and the most well-known cryptocurrency out there, but it is definitely not the only one.
In the past few years, there has been a rise in the amount of decentralized finance platforms out there. Each one is competing to be the best one and offer the most comprehensive solutions. When there is competition like that, it is easy to see that this is something that is wanted in society and can provide a solution to the problem that has been put forward in regard to centralized finances.
There are 2,000 different cryptocurrencies in the world today, and is likely to increase over the next few years. This is why it is so important to understand decentralized finance and what it entails. There is no indication that this is a phase or something that will pass. These platforms are growing bigger and more people are putting their confidence in them. This is a new way of doing finance and it is winning the world over, little by little.
What Is Decentralized Finance?
In its simplest form decentralized finance is a type of financial trading that does not require a middleman. Anyone can buy and transact with financial products completely on their own. This means any form of identification like an ID document, proof of residence, bank account, or social security number is not needed to use the system. This creates security in the fact that the transactions are anonymous. Hackers and thieves have a hard time targeting someone to steal from because there is no way of knowing who they are in the system.
Through the use of a technology called blockchain, which will be explained in more detail in the next chapter, it allows people to transact directly to each other. This means that people will be able to get their money faster and will not be subject to the rules and regulations imposed upon them by a bank. Now, this does not mean that there are no rules to the system. There are, and you also have to abide by the rules and regulations of the country you live in. In this regard, it is not completely removed from the rules of society.
It has taken quite a while to get to the point where decentralized finance is a well-known option and one that actually works well in society. There are multiple technologies involved and many different protocols that had to be put in place in order for it to work. In order for a decentralized system to work, it has to be made up of a mix of open-source technologies, blockchain, and proprietary software (Sharma, 2021a).
In order for decentralized finance to work the way it does, it needs to make use of smart contracts. A smart contract is one that is written in between lines of code. These types of contracts are self-executing which means there is no need to print, sign, and send them off. The coding of the system is what controls the execution of the contracts and the transactions that have been made are easily tracked and they are irreversible. The use of smart contracts is what enables the users to trust the transactions that take place and be carried out among the anonymous users of the system. This allows the system and the transactions to operate completely separate from a large central authority that has to validate and regulate the transactions that take place (Frankenfield, 2021a).
This seems like a very advanced form of technology that would be allowed to permit the use of smart contracts. However, it was first proposed in 1994 by a computer scientist named Nick Szabo. He was the inventor of Bit Gold, which was one of the first forms of virtual currency ever created and actually one of the foundations of the very popular Bitcoin. It is easy to think that Bitcoin was the first ever cryptocurrency to be thought of and developed, but people have been toying with the idea for years. This might also be the reason that the world seems to be more ready than ever to move over to a crypto way of selling and purchasing goods and services.
Nick Szabo’s idea wasn’t exactly like what we have today. His original idea was to extend the point of sale transaction methods to a more digital way of doing things. He wrote a few papers on the subject and one of them stated how he saw the technology being used in the future. He basically stated that this use of smart contracts could be used to turn the complex processes of transactions into standardized contracts and that would allow for much lower transaction costs. If we look at how cryptocurrency is used now, his predictions have pretty much come true (Frankenfield, 2021a).
While we are living in exciting times where decentralized finance is growing and can clearly be seen as a solution for many problems that relate to how we transact now, we must not forget that this system is still being built upon. With this being said, due to the measures that have been put in place by the inventors and creators of the systems that support decentralized finances, the regulation and risk of oversight is almost nonexistent. Many people are predicting that the future of finance is decentralization. This would change the face of finance as we know it as we would no longer need backs and brokerages to handle our money (Sharma, 2021a).
Technology has played a big role in finances for a long time now. We use technology to pay at point of sale devices, we use it when we take out or deposit money into ATMs, and we use technology when we use banking apps on our phones. So it is not that much of a surprise that the role of technology in finances would be increasing. We see the role of technology being integrated into almost every area of our lives. It is making things much more convenient and safer for us. It is really not a far-fetched idea to believe that technology would play a major role in how we handle and use our finances.
As it stands, technology has been kept at bay when it comes to finances. It is only being used as a tool to facilitate the movement of our money. When we transact with our money, that is when we engage with the technological side of it. In the background, the banks and companies are dealing with all the legal issues, current standards, financial markets, and any international laws that will allow the transaction to take place. This explains why it can take a few days to authorize a transaction, and even longer if it is an international transaction. This is why technology has to take a back seat, there are so many other things to deal with when it comes to finances.
One of the major positives of decentralized finances is that technology is at the forefront and all of those other things have been dealt with by building solutions into the code. Currently, when anyone talks about decentralized finances, they are only referring to blockchain and cryptocurrencies. However, this is not the full scope of it.
Currently finances are heavily centralized and this is not only limited to banks. There are major economic hubs in countries all over the world that function as the financial hubs of the country as well as the economic hub. This means that these cities or states influence the economy of the country quite heavily. Some of these economic hubs are London in the UK, New York in the USA, Mumbai in India, and Shanghai in China. Not only do these cities play a major role in the economy of their own country but that of the rest of the world.
A country’s economy is interdependent on a whole lot of factors, and that includes how bad or how good another country’s economy is doing. For example, if the U.S. dollar weakens, it in turn makes a whole lot of third world countries’ currencies much stronger. This has nothing to do with the economic capabilities of that third world country, but they are reaping some benefits from the economic drop in the USA. If the U.S. dollar were to gain the confidence of investors and therefore increase in value, all of those third world countries’ currency would drop in value. Now this does not mean that the economic capabilities and production has lessened, it has just suffered a ripple effect from what is happening in the USA.
You will also notice when there is some sort of political unrest or a change in power, the U.S. dollar will drop in value. This may not be for a long time, but there is some instability in the economy because of what is happening in politics. The economy is usually functioning exactly the same as it always has, and even if the output is exactly the same, the other factors that affect the finances of the country are coming into play even if they have no direct impact to the economy at that moment.
The whole world’s finances are interdependent with each other. This is the model that is copied by the financial service industry. That is why you will find that they have branches, headquarters, investments, and partnerships in economic hubs all over the world. Just based on that there are a whole host of laws and jurisdictions that apply when it comes to finance. It is also why most people just don’t understand finance. It is an incredibly complicated process to wrap your mind around, so people rather just trust their banks to handle everything and they just see the end product.
This model has been in place for the last century. It has worked well for us so far, but the Great Recession and the recessions after that have shown that there is a flaw in the architecture of how we do things. When this happened the whole world hit a global recession because everything is tied together. This can be a dangerous problem to be left with, especially for the countries that did not actually have a problem before the recession. It’s like you don’t actually have any control over your money, because as soon as a recession hits it drops the value of what you originally had. This can create some major problems as we saw in 2007.
The goal of decentralized finances is to remove the institutions who control the money from the mix. The end user has more control over their money and they don’t have to put their finances in the hands of someone else. The end user also has no risk of being discriminated against by age, ethnicity, or any other personal bias that may be caused by having to go through a central authority. Decentralized finance offers the same things as regular financial institutes and also offers their own solutions based on what is needed by the system. The trading and transaction services that are offered with a decentralized system specifically cater to the user instead of an institution who has to authorize and handle the money of the user.
What Makes Up Decentralized Finance?
Decentralized finance has components that almost mimic that of the existing financial climates. Every component of the system is found in a software stack. These components are layered in the stack and they perform specific functions that allow the system to work and function properly. Essentially the components of each layer will be able to be composed together so that form a decentralized finance stack.
There are four layers that make up a stack in a decentralized finance system: A settlement layer, a protocol layer, an application layer, and an aggregation layer. We will talk about them one by one so that you can get a good understanding of what they are, their functions, and how they relate to the system as a whole.
The settlement layer is the base layer of which the system is built on. It is often referred to as level zero for this very reason. This layer is made up of the public blockchain and the digital currency that belongs to the system. The transactions that take place on the decentralized finance app will be settled and confirmed using the digital currency found in the system. This is the layer in which most of the transactions take place. If it is a Bitcoin system you would be able to transact with Bitcoin, if it was Ethereum then you would transact with ethers and so on.
The next layer is the protocol layer, where the software protocols can be found. The software protocols are what govern the activities and tasks that take place in the system. The standards and rules of the protocols have been written for this purpose. The standards and rules of an industry would have been agreed upon by all users who operate in that industry, and it would be a prerequisite to actually operating in that industry. Decentralized finances are not limited by the amount of entities that can use the protocols to develop a system or an app at the same time. This means that many different people or entities can be on and build a service of their own should they want to. This layer helps to provide the system with liquidity in the financial ecosystem that has been created by decentralized finances.
The third layer is the application layer. This is the customer facing layer and holds everything that a user would be interacting with on a normal day on the system. This layer takes the more complicated protocols and changes them into something that is simple for the end user to interact with and understand. Most users don’t really need to understand all the inner workings of the systems in order to use it. This is the same with the regular financial institution that we have today. Most of us are not aware of what goes on behind the scenes, or understand how it works, but we are all able to use a simpler version of it that is put forward to us. The most common functions and applications of a decentralized finance system can be found on this layer. Some of these are the exchange of cryptocurrency and lending services.
The forth, and final, layer is the aggregation layer. An aggregator is a program that links or displays related items or data. It first has to collect all the relevant information before linking them up. This layer connects the various different applications from the application layer and can then provide a service to investors. The investor might then be able to do a transfer from two different financial instruments. This will allow them to maximize their returns in a way that they would not be able to originally do. If this was to be done in a normal financial system, it would result in a whole lot of paperwork and coordination from different departments. This will most likely result in it taking a really long time for the transfers and transaction to be approved and on top of that there would probably be some additional fees attached to the process. With decentralized finance, everything is technology based, which means that the process can be smoothed out and this will allow the traders and investors to switch between the different services quite seamlessly. An example of a service that can be found on this layer is lending and borrowing. There are many others that can be found on the aggregation level, but it will also depend on the system being used (Sharma, 2021).

Chapter 3:
The Ins and Outs of Blockchain
If you are trying to wrap your head around cryptocurrency, you will need to know all about blockchain and how it works. It is the fundamental way in which the systems have been designed. Satoshi Nakamoto was the inventor of blockchain. He was also the brains behind the biggest cryptocurrency out there, Bitcoin.
Although Nakamoto technically invented blockchain technology, he was not the first to have the idea. Stuart Haber and W. Scott Stornetta wanted to make a system that would allow them to add timestamps to documents and not have them tampered with. This was all the way back in 1991. Nakamoto used blockchain to create the transparent ledger system that Bitcoin is built on. Anyone can use blockchain to house any kind of data and information. The uses for it do not only lie in numerical data.
There are many other companies and programs that are being developed that use this blockchain system. This will significantly change the way we do things, because it means that systems will better be able to handle data and it will be more secure. If implemented in the right way blockchain can actually be developed to let people vote from home in presidential and council elections. This will eliminate a lot of human error in processes, like counting votes. The uses for this technology far exceed what we can see today. Cryptocurrency was just the first to use it.
What Is Blockchain?
In the simplest of terms, blockchain is just a database. However, it doesn’t end here. There are a lot of differences with blockchain and other forms of databases, but first let’s understand what a database is. Most people have a general idea of what a database is even if they have never worked with one. It is simply just a collection of information that is stored on a server or computer. If you need to find a certain piece of data you can search it and get what you are looking for. It is usually structured as a table so that the user can find what they are looking for with ease.
Reading this it is easy to mistake a database for a spreadsheet. A database is a little more complicated than that. A spreadsheet is designed to be used for one person or perhaps a small group of people. There is a limited amount of people that can access it and there is only a limited amount of information that can be stored on it. A database can be accessed by a large number of users at one time. The information that can be stored on a database can be significantly larger and have a variety in the information type.
The only way you can get away with storing large amounts of data and allowing lots of people to access it, is to have everything on a server. A server is allowed to run by computers that have significant power to handle all this information. There are various sized servers and it all depends on how many people need to access the information and how much information has to be stored. Small to medium sized companies can have small servers that can handle their day to day information and give access to the employees and clients, but bigger companies like Facebook and Google need to have massive servers to handle all the information being poured into it and being pulled up. These companies handle information for the whole world so this is understandable.
When it comes to a traditional database, it is completely controlled by a certain company or person. They have control of the data and how it should be used. If there is something that Google does not want to show up on their search engine they can remove it. They have control over how the information is organized and they are the only ones that have access to everything that goes on behind the scenes. This is where blockchain differs from other databases. It also stores the information, but this information is available to everyone who is on the system and no one person has power over the data. This means that nothing can be deleted or altered for any reason.
The way blockchain systems store data is completely different to the way other databases do it. Above we mentioned that a database typically stores data on a table for ease of finding and editing information. A blockchain database collects information and groups them. These groups are referred to as blocks. Only a certain amount of data can be on one block after the limit is reached and the block is closed up and no other information can be added to it. Once the block of information is filled, it gets chained up to the previous block of information, hence the name blockchain. All the information that comes after must be placed in a new block and then the process repeats itself (Conway, 2020).
The way this is structured means that it forms an irreversible chain of data. Once the block has been filled up and links up to the previous chain it cannot be altered. The blocks are also stamped with a time so that it shows the exact time it was added to the chain. This process created the environment to have a decentralized network. When it comes to cryptocurrency, everything can be traced back to the exact minutes the transactions happened and who made the transaction. This keeps everyone accountable for their own transactions and it prevents fraud or other financial crimes.
With Bitcoin, the blockchain acts as a referral point for every block of data. If there is an error the transaction can be cross-referenced with the thousands of other blocks until the discrepancy is found. Let’s say one of the users try to tamper with the data in one of the blocks, this will cause the system to cross-reference with all the other blocks to find out which one is the incorrect block. The system will then alter the information in that block so that it is correct. This kind of process allows blockchain to run as a decentralized system. It can correct and pick out errors on its own. With cryptocurrency, blockchain holds mainly numerical information but it can also be used to hold legal contracts, inventories, and IDs (Conway, 2020).
This kind of technology allows all information to be transparently viewed. Anyone who has a personal node or a blockchain explorer can view all the transactions as they happen. Anyone can track what is going on with Bitcoin at any point, from anywhere. The Bitcoin system allows everything to be anonymous, but every single Bitcoin can be traced and tracked. If someone were to hack the Bitcoin system and steal the Bitcoin, the system would be able to find the missing Bitcoin when someone tries to spend it or use it for any other purpose.
Blockchain is a very secure system, because the developers have made sure to account for security and trust issues. As the blocks get added to the chain they move into a specific position on the chain. The position is called a height, which basically just labels the position it is on the chain. Once the block has been added and ‘stamped’ it is very difficult to go back and make a change. If a change needs to be made there needs to be a vote to which the majority will have to go in favor of the change. If not, the change is not accepted and it has to stay how it is. This prevents anyone going in and changing or tampering with the system to benefit themselves.
Each of the blocks contains a hash, which is connected to the hash of the block before it, along with the timestamp. This hash creates a string of numbers that forms a code that links everything together in a specific way. We will go into more detail about a hash and how it works in the next section. The simple version is that if someone changes the data in a block, it changes that block’s hash. Then it will no longer align with everyone else’s copy of data. The thieves’ version of the Bitcoin that they stole will then be thrown out and not be able to be used.
In order for any hacker to succeed in changing the data in a block so that they can steal it, they have to make their version the majority so that the system accepts it. The system works fast to find any issues so in order to pull this off the hacker will have to change 51% of the copies in the chain at the same time. This is a mammoth task, so unless someone has a massive amount of money to waste there is almost no chance that this would ever happen. If someone has that much money and wants to have Bitcoin it would make a lot more sense for them to just buy it with that money.
What Is a Hash?
We mentioned the term hash is the previous section. It would be understandable to be confused about what it is or what function it performs if you are not familiar with coding and technological terms in general. In order to understand how blockchain works, you would need to understand a hash and its function in the whole process.
The simplest and shortest definition of a hash is that it is a function that takes numbers and letters and converts it into an encrypted output of a fixed length. In order for cryptocurrency to work and be a viable alternative to money, it needs blockchain. All the information that is validated through the blockchain is done so through the use of a hash, this results in an encrypted value that does look anything like the original information (Frankenfield, 2020a).
If you wanted to mine cryptocurrency you would need to work with the hash. Hashing is just the way in which the data needs to be processed. A mathematical equation is used and the resulting output is of a certain length, this length is fixed across the board. The fixed output of the hash is actually a security feature. Regardless of the length of the information put in the output, it will always have the same length. This means that anyone looking at the hashed information will never be able to tell how long the original information is.
We already know that blockchains work by creating blocks of information and linking them together. Each one of these blocks has a number that is attached to it. This number will only be used once in the whole blockchain and is therefore unique. When someone is mining they focus on this number. The miner basically has to figure out what string of numbers is used in this block. All this is, is trial and error because there are so many different ways the numbers could be put together. The ‘answer’ to the problem is called the target hash. If the miner is able to get the exact answer or one that is lower than that, it is deemed as correct and the miner has mined successfully. The more difficult it took to create the hash will determine how long it will take to solve it.
To make this simpler to understand. If there were two separate inputs one of them being “Hi” and the other one being “I am going outside to walk the dog,” the hash would produce an output of the same length for both of them, but they would both be different outputs. So if you want to input “Hi,” you will always get the same output. If there are even minor changes made to the hash, it will change it completely and link to another input if there is one to match it. This discrepancy will be caught out by the blockchains cross referencing checks.
The Benefits of Blockchain
Blockchain technology is spreading into many other areas and is not just limited to cryptocurrency. This will significantly change the way things are done in many industries. To fully understand this shift and what makes blockchain so attractive, we must understand the benefits of this technology.
It Is Accurate
Blockchain is an incredibly accurate way of documenting data. Its decentralized nature allows it to run on thousands of computers and all transactions and changes have to be approved by the entire network. Since everything has to be cross-referenced throughout the whole network, it makes picking up mistakes much easier. Once a mistake or error is picked up the system makes the changes so that it matches up with the rest of the copies of the blockchain. The system does away with human authorization, which is subject to human error. Not even the smartest and the most meticulous person can catch every single error.
It Allows for Privacy Better Than Any Other System
Privacy is a big thing in today’s age. Keeping someone anonymous is the big draw in this system. Usually if you send money or information to someone you have to identify yourself. Banks usually pass on some sort of information to the person receiving the funds. With blockchain, you are able to see every detail of the transaction, but it keeps the person behind it anonymous. Every person has a user ID so that transactions can be traced back to that identification without the user's real world identity having to be compromised, unless they tell the recipient that they are sending them something. It is not an anonymous system, but it is completely confidential.
When someone makes a transaction what is recorded by the blockchain is the user’s public key not any of their personal information. Of course, the person’s blockchain address is linked to their identity. However this does not show up on the system. All your personal information will not be revealed no matter what transactions you make on the system.
It Is Far Cheaper
Companies and individual people are always looking for ways to cut down costs. Although blockchain technology will take some initial investment and maintenance costs, in the long run this is much cheaper. In most companies there are various steps that need to be taken to get things verified and approved. With blockchain, you can remove all the middlemen and just get the verification without all the extra steps. It is a far better investment than making sure you send things off to the relevant parties for verification or approval. This also means that things will get done quicker and more efficiently.
People Who Don’t Have Access to Banks Can Use This System
Blockchain can also be used to help those who don’t have access to banks. In many developing countries they still run mainly using cash. After a day’s work the employee will get paid with cash, this makes it difficult for them to build up wealth. They have to store their savings in their house and this is easily stolen which means that they are at a serious disadvantage. There are over two billion adults that don’t have access to any banking facilities, so this is a real problem and makes it even harder for them to climb out of poverty.
The usage of cryptocurrency and blockchain means that they do not have to have access to a bank in order to store up their wealth. Just an internet connection, which is far easier to get to people than building and establishing banking systems in those countries. Most developing countries have access to the internet, even if it is only limited.
If Bitcoin moves to those countries, the people who live there only need to keep their Bitcoin wallet keys with them. This can be written down on a piece of paper, kept on a cellphone, or just memorized. Even if they have to write it down on a piece of paper, this is far easier to keep hidden than a pile of cash.
Blockchain can also be used for many other things like keeping track of medical records, contracts, and property rights. This will help with keeping track of citizens and making sure that everyone is getting the right attention and nothing unfair is going on. Where there are no systems to dictate what belongs to who, it is easy to steal and get away with it. Implementing these blockchain systems can do a lot to fight corruption and help better the lives of the people living in countries that are still developing.
Security and Transparency
We have already spoken about why this system is so secure. Every transaction has to be verified by the blockchain network. Anything that happens must match up to the thousands of computers that start working on confirming the transaction. It is incredibly difficult to commit fraud or steal Bitcoin unless you truly have a lot of money to append on the endeavor. At this point, if you are trying to steal Bitcoin you are probably doing it for fun because the cost of doing that will very easily exceed the cost of the Bitcoin you steal. This is one of the aspects that make the system so secure. Even if something was stolen, the whole system is so transparent that network members would quickly see that there has been in the chain. They will then fork off to another chain and the hacked chain would drop in value as a result. This system not only makes it extremely hard to steal or hack into the system, it makes it far more attractive to just buy Bitcoin than wasting the money on trying any funny business.
Any person can see the code that runs the system. This is one of the most transparent systems that exist. If someone wants to audit the system they can go ahead at any point. This keeps accountability at a high level since every person can see all transactions and changes. The transparency allows there to not be one person that controls the system. Any changes have to be OK’d by the majority of users in order for it to happen. This makes sure that any alterations made will be beneficial for all or the majority of the users.

Chapter 4:
The History of Money
Cryptocurrency seems as it will be the new form of currency all over the world. Some people are skeptical and even scared about this future because it is so new and seems like unchartered territory. However, the same principles of money have always been around. Currency has always changed and evolved. Thousands of years ago, people used pieces of silver to trade with and slowly that evolved until we came to the paper money we use today. If we take a look at how money has developed we will see that paper money is not even the most popular form of money we use today. Most of us use our cards to buy items with. This is not physical money that is able to be counted in our hands. Instead, we choose to trust numbers on a screen to tell us how much money we have at our disposal. This is just more convenient for most people and it seems safer than carrying around a whole wad of cash wherever we go. Since we have already moved towards a more digitized form of currency, is it really that far of a leap to think that cryptocurrency is the next monetary form? Before we get into all of that let’s dive into the history of money and where it all came from.
Where It All Started
Money as we know it actually has no real value. The value of a currency is determined by humans. If we really wanted to we could replace all the paper money in the world with shells and that could be the new form of currency. In fact, back when money started there were various objects that people used to trade with and one of those objects was shells. Money is just symbolic of a certain value and it is a medium of exchange. We need money in order to communicate the price of certain items and then exchange for what we need or want. This has always been the goal of currency, but it has had many different looks over the years. For the last 3,000 years money and currency has played some function in human civilization. As the years go on, how people look at currency and what they use to trade with has evolved dramatically.
If we go back to the beginning, when people used to live in small villages and communities, we will find the first indications of using currency to trade. But before any form of money people used to do a direct trade for what they wanted. Small communities ran on trade jobs. So there would be farmers, bakers, carpenters, and seamstresses. Every person would have something that another would want. Since a baker could not live off just eating bread they would trade their bread to the farmer for a chicken or eggs. The same would be for any person looking to get something else that they needed.
The problem with this way of doing things is that there is no exact value to the things that you are trading. If the farmer did not want bread, then the baker would not have anything to trade with and would not be able to get the items that they needed. You would then have to go to multiple farmers to find someone who would think the bread is a good enough trade for the eggs. This would take a really long time to get the items that you needed. The other problem is that every farmer would place a different value on their eggs. So the baker would not know how many loaves of bread they would need in order to trade with the farmer until they get there. Even if you traded one loaf of bread for two eggs last week, the farmer could easily just refuse it this week and ask for two loaves of bread for the same two eggs.
You can see how this becomes an ineffective way to trade. Imagine doing this in today’s world. It could easily take weeks for someone to get a bottle of milk or some other essential item. As communities grew, it just became harder and harder to do this because it was a very difficult system to regulate.
Eventually people decided that it wasn’t working so they placed value on certain items to use as currency. Back in the day this could have been anything depending on the community. People used stones, feathers, shells, salt, animal tusks, and basically anything else that they deemed fit. You can easily pick out how this could cause a problem. Since these items were so easy to come by people could just go to the beach and pick up some shells or find a stone outside their house. This meant that it was very difficult to place value on these items because new ones could just be added even if no work or trade had been done to get them. On top of that, since every community would have a different item to represent currency you could not trade with people from other communities. This meant that people inland could use stones as their currency, but when they went to the coastal communities and wanted to trade for fish the coastal communities would use shells as their currency. So the stones would mean nothing to them. What was needed was an overall standard form of currency that could be used across the board.
The First Standard Currency
It is important to note that most communities around the world used the form of trade spoken about above. It was not necessarily something that was invented by any one people group. It just seemed like a natural progression when civilizations became more modern. One of the first civilizations that had a standard form of measurement when it came to money was the Ancient Babylonians. They used something called shekels which were just pieces of metal. If you wanted to buy something, you would have to weigh up your shekels in order to trade it for a specific item.
There were other people groups that started to move away from bartering to get what they needed. One of the most notable changes was after 700 B.C., the Chinese decided that using usable objects as a form of currency was not working because they were too big to carry around. It made the transport of currency too difficult. So they made miniature replicas of the objects that would be traded. These were replicas of things like tools and weapons.
After a while, they realized this wasn’t going to work, because having a large amount of tiny axes or arrows in your bag or pocket could be quite dangerous. This is when they changed these objects into the form of a circle. These were far easier to carry around and they would not stab you when you reached into your bag to pay for something. Eventually this type of currency spread across China and then to other parts of the world. The country now known as turkey was the first to have minted currency. The coins were made from a mixture of silver and gold, and were stamped all sorts of different pictures that meant different things.
Again at the forefront of currency changes the Chinese moved over from coins to paper currency. The big driving force of this being the fact that carrying around a large amount of coins is a tiresome business. There is also speculation about a metal shortage, which would make the move to paper currency make more sense to the Chinese rulers. The rulers would keep all the gold and silver in the castle with them and issue something like IOU documents that represented the actual gold and silver coins. They were the only people group that were using paper money for a long time.
The Chinese were able to perfect the use of this currency and then when Marco Polo visited China and saw how they were using the paper money, he documented it and it made its way back to Europe. However, the use of paper money in Europe would take a few hundred years to actually catch on. In this time, Europe became incredibly rich due to their exploring and taking the precious metals from other lands. They were able to mint coins in large quantities because of their various land conquests.
Eventually, the coins became too much for people to carry around and they too opted for paper money. They would deposit their coins into a back and get the paper banknotes as a representation of what they had just deposited. This is the point where things start to look like how they do now. The only big difference between how they did things in the 1600 to 1700s and now is that paper money was issued through the banks and other private institutions. Now this is all controlled and regulated by the government of the country instead of individual entities.
The Gold Exchange Standard
Humans have always been fascinated with gold. Its beauty, malleability, and scarcity are what predominantly captured the hearts of humans. People have had wars over this metal. It is then no surprise that the value of gold has always been quite high. It is not because gold has any intrinsic value, but because humans have placed such a high value on it. This has carried out through the years. Even now people place a high value on gold and choose to wear it as a form of ornate jewelry often speaking to the wealth of the person wearing it. As you can see, history plays a big part in how we see things in the present. If there wasn’t such a huge emphasis placed on gold in the past the chances are that we would just look at it as another metal.
If you read through the history books, you will see that gold was used for jewelry and even worship. People placed such a high value on gold that they would make statues of their gods from it so they could worship them. As time went on, gold changed in use and people traded with gold coins. Since it was used as a form of currency, the more you had the richer you were. This is what started the gold rushes we see throughout history. The first gold rush happened in America in the 15th century. This brought many settlers to the country. Spain plundered a lot of it in the 16th century and that increased Europe’s supply of gold by five times its initial amount. This meant that Europe was a power player due to the fact it was the richest nation in the world. Gold was found in other countries such as Australia, South Africa, and other parts of America. These gold rushes happened in the 19th century. All the gold was brought back to the European nations that plundered it.
As discussed above, eventually carrying around gold coins became troublesome and paper money was introduced. Paper money and gold were used in some ways simultaneously and that caused a struggle between the two forms of currency. In the early 1800s, both Europe and America adopted the gold standard as the ultimate solution to the currency and inflation issues they were facing. Around the 1870s, a majority of the other countries joined the gold standard, making it a solid world-wide standard. The gold standard remained the way to go. The classical gold standard is known as the period between 1880 to 1914.
The gold standard was a system that linked a country’s currency to gold. Countries would committee to fix their currencies to a specific amount of gold. This was a fairly stable way of doing things and reduced the amount of inflation that happened even if new gold was found and added to a country’s wealth. You can see how stable the gold standard was by the fact that the United States set their standard to $20.67 per ounce of gold in 1834 and it remained the same until 1933. That is 99 years of having a currency that did not inflate or change. Since most countries had set their currency to the gold standard it allowed international trade to boom. Economic growth and trade were at a point that had not ever been seen in history before that point.
During this time countries worked together better than they ever had. It was a simple rule that allowed inflation and deflation to be curbed. However, as we know, this did not last forever. The collapse of the gold standard started during World War I. It caused a massive change in the political climate in the world and alliances were changing. Countries started to go into debt and the finances that governments had started to deteriorate. While the gold standard was a great system that held through the good times, it had a tough time staying grounded through the hard times. Governments started losing confidence in the gold standard and it became clear that the world needed something more flexible to handle their finances with. Another problem was that the amount of gold that was available was falling to a level that was below the fast-paced growth of the economy. This was creating limits for the countries that held fast to the gold standard.
The gold standard was abandoned by the British government in 1931, and by 1933, the United States quickly followed. There were still remnants of the system throughout most of the 1900s. This is due to the Bretton Woods Agreement that was put in place and followed until 1971. This agreement stated that all currencies would be valued against the U.S. dollar and the dollar, in turn, could be converted into gold. By the end of World War II about 75% of the world’s gold was in the United States. Now the world had to build itself back up after the war and the gold reserves started to drop as money had to go to the war-torn countries and America had its own high import demand which had to be funded. Since the gold reserves were diminishing and there was a fear that other countries would want to redeem their assets for gold, this way of handling money was not going to work. In 1973, all remaining pieces of the gold standard were removed from the way countries handled their currency and a fiat system of dealing with money was adopted (Lioudis, 2021).
Fiat Currencies
After all these events took place that led to the downfall of the gold standard, something else had to take its place. This is where fiat currency came into being. Fiat currency is one that is government issued and is not backed by any kind of physical commodity. The value of this kind of currency is determined by the supply and demand, as well as the stability of the government that has issued it. You will see that the currencies go up and down depending on what goes on in the country. Often when there is a change in power or president then there is an unsteadiness in the strength of that country’s finances. All major global currencies are now fiat currencies, this includes the dollar, pound, euro, yen, and peso. These are known as representative money, as it represents a claim on a commodity that can be redeemed for that commodity (Corporate Finance Institute, n.d.).
While fiat money was made popular in the 20th century when the gold standard collapsed, this was not the first time fiat money was invented. China was the first country to introduce fiat currency all the way back in 1000 AD. They faced the same situation as the rest of the world did 900 years later, where they were unable to use metal coins due to a shortage in the metal. When countries come to the point where they need to protect their precious metals they opt for fiat currencies. In 1971, Richard Nixon abandoned the idea of the gold standard and made a change in the United States economics. Most other countries followed suit. As explained earlier, fiat currency works based on the government of the country. It only has value if the government maintains that value or if the parties that are involved in a transaction agree upon the value. The word ‘fiat’ comes from Latin roots and it means “let it be done.”
Fiat money works now because it allows the economy to grow indefinitely without being stifled by any physical material. The economy around the world has grown and expanded at an incredibly fast pace over the past few decades. Central banks have a much greater control over the money, credit, liquidity, and assets. There are some negatives to fiat money that are important to take note of. Since there is an unlimited supply of fiat money. Just because the money is unlimited does not mean that the banks have any power to stop possessions or financial depressions. It also means that there is a risk of creating bubbles in the economy, like the housing bubble in 2007.
One example of how fiat money can go wrong if not managed properly is the hyperinflation that happened in Zimbabwe. This is definitely a worst-case scenario, but it is something that did happen and it had huge economic impacts on the country. In the early 2000s, the government of Zimbabwe responded to the country’s economic situation by printing more money. They printed money at such a fast pace that it resulted in hyperinflation in the country. The more money that was printed the higher merchants had to hike up their prices in order to make a profit. Prices were rising at such a speed that it was almost unbelievable, some days the prices would double overnight. Eventually, people had to carry around buckets of money so that they could just pay for basic things like bread and milk. At the height of this economic crisis the Zimbabwean dollar stood 100-trillion to one U.S. Dollar. At that point the Zimbabwean currency had to be scraped and they used the U.S. dollar to trade with (Chen, 2021).
Fiat currency has worked for most countries if the government is able to handle their economy well. There have been other countries that have faced economic crises, not to the extent of Zimbabwe but it is still pretty bad. Another example of this is Venezuela, where a cup of coffee costs 1.55 million bolivares. Eventually many people opted to start trading with U.S. dollars. The country is now trying to move towards using a national cryptocurrency called the petro.
Where We Are Now
Currently we are still using fiat currency for all of our transactions. There have been changes in how we trade with this currency. Instead of using paper money, many people have moved over to just card transactions. This is a safer option than carrying around large amounts of cash on your person. Banks keep and regulate the money that belongs to the people. They work on a lending and investing principle so technically if you want to go and visit your money on any random day, there will be nothing physical for you to see at the bank. The money that you can see when you check your account balance is probably being lent to someone else and when you want to take your money, it will be the money someone else put into the bank. This does not mean that the banks are spending or wasting your money, but they are moving it around as they see fit.
In this day in age you can still buy and sell gold and gold coins, but this is not used for everyday purchases. You can expect to get funny looks from merchants if you try and pay for your bread and milk with a gold coin. However, people still do trade these, but it is more for the purpose of attaining collectables or having a speculative asset.
Since the economy and currency is always changing it would be foolish to think that we have made it. There will always be new innovations that come about that allow us to do things more easily and hopefully safer as well. Right now we can see a push towards using cryptocurrency. This type of decentralized finance takes the power away from the bank and governments and places it in the hands of the user. The way money works now, we are forced to just trust the government and the banks without money. There is no other option so we do it. Giving people the option to have more control over their assets is an incredibly desirable offer. While we are not at a point where cryptocurrency is the main source of dealing with money, we can expect to see a rise in its use. Who knows what the future will hold in terms of currency and how we view it.

Chapter 5:
Bitcoin—The Most Famous of the Cryptocurrencies
Bitcoin was first created in 2009 and since then it has become the world’s top digital currency. At the time of writing this book, in 2021, Bitcoin has seen an incredible rise by about 400%. This means that it is sitting at one bitcoin to 50,000 dollars. With this rise in popularity and value, Bitcoin is peaking the interest of more and more people. Understanding Bitcoin and all the intricate details has become even more important than ever before.
The Founder and Creator of Bitcoin
Satoshi Nakamoto is the name you will tie to the creation of Bitcoin. If you do a Google search this name is the one that will pop up. However, the story is not that simple. The truth is that we still don’t know who the creator of Bitcoin really is. This leaves us with many questions, including who is Satoshi Nakamoto?
The first traces of Satoshi Nakamoto were found in a white paper published in 2008. The paper was titled “Bitcoin: A Peer-to-Peer Electronic Cash System.” The paper was sent out through a cryptography mailing list. This is the reason why the name Satoshi Nakamoto has been tied to the creation of Bitcoin for all these years. It has since been found out that this is just a pseudonym created by the real founder of the cryptocurrency.
As Bitcoin began to gain popularity, people were more desperate and curious to find out who this Satoshi Nakamoto really was. This is naturally we all want to be able to put a face to the name, especially when the person has founded something so groundbreaking. All of a sudden in 2011, Satoshi Nakamoto vanished off the face of the earth. The only explanation for this was a short email sent to one of Bitcoin core developers, Mike Hearn. The email simply stated that he was moving on to different things and that Bitcoin was in good hands. Now this cryptic message was not satisfactory for the vast majority of people and the search for him still continued.
Eventually, the name Satoshi Nakamoto was tied to the face of a now 70-year-old Asian man who lived in Los Angeles with his mother. Satoshi Nakamoto was his name at birth, but now he goes by Dorian S. Nakamoto. He has publicly stated that he is not the creator of Bitcoin, nor has he had anything to do with its development. He was followed by reporters for weeks since people did not believe that he was lying and trying to throw them off the trail. Dorian Nakamoto was featured in a Newsweek article stating he was the creator due to his background in engineering. This was denied by Dorian and he issued a statement to Newsweek saying so. A few weeks after that, there was a post on a public forum on a website called P2P foundation from Satoshi Nakamoto that simply stated, “I am not Dorian Nakamoto.”
Since then, there have been many theories about who the real Satoshi Nakamoto is. Names like Craig Wright, Nick Szabo, and Elon Musk have all made the list. They have all denied that they were the creator of Bitcoin. There are still many theories going around and every person who has been thought to be the creator has denied it or made no comment on it. It is clear that whoever is the creator of this cryptocurrency does not want to be in the public eye.
There have been many people that are criticizing the Bitcoin creator for remaining anonymous. However, there are some good reasons to stay under the radar. In the past, people who have invented other forms of currency have been charged with various legal consequences including house arrest. There are also some who say that if the real identity of Satoshi Nakamoto becomes known then the value of Bitcoin would seriously plummet. One of the major reasons being that the whole premise of Bitcoin is to transact with autonomy. You cannot be linked to your real-world self when you trade with Bitcoin. Revealing their identity would defeat the purpose of this in a sense. The mystery also creates a sense of intrigue, which is why there is so much talk around it.
Even though there is a lot of mystery surrounding who this person is, there are some things that we do know. Obviously, this person is a genius. There is no way that an average person would be able to create a system so sophisticated. Whoever this person is has a higher than average IQ and is well versed in math and coding. The next thing that we can deduce is that this person is not American and possibly not Japanese. The reason we can guess this is because the way this person writes alludes to the fact that they are a fluent English speaker. If they Japanese it would be less likely that they would communicate with such proficiency in the English language. The spellings and terms used by this person is more British than American English. Although there are many English-speaking countries that use British English over American, there is strong evidence that this person does live in the UK because when they post it is usually during the UK’s daylight hours. We also can’t rule out that there is more than one person behind the mask. There could very possibly be a large team of developers who have sat down and created this technology.
Most of what we know is just speculation based on the evidence that we have. There is nothing completely solid and it could turn out that everyone is completely wrong. The truth is that we will never know who Satoshi Nakamoto is until he or she steps forward and provides concrete proof that they are the real creator of Bitcoin. However, it is highly unlikely that this person would come forward. It has been over a decade since its creation and release and they have continued to stay anonymous. If they wanted to be known, then the chances are they would have come forward by now.
The world will always be curious to find out who this person is, but sometimes not knowing is better. Since we really don’t have another alternative we just have to settle that we might never know who the real creator of Bitcoin is. This does not take away anything from this brilliant form of currency. The creator still has a large net worth and owns a large chunk of the currency. They are clearly still making money on it, so there are not really any pressing reasons for them to come forward.
What Is Bitcoin?
Bitcoin is a cryptocurrency, which means that there are no physical Bitcoins anywhere in the world. The whole system works by code. Bitcoins are kept track of using a public ledger where you can see where Bitcoin is moving around. Everyone has access to this information so there is no chance of any funny business going on. This ledger makes it very difficult to reverse transactions or make fake ones. Due to this, there is little to no fraud risk when dealing with Bitcoin. It is completely separate from the banks. This adds an element of safety because you do not have to rely on the bank to keep or invest your money. You can never be fully certain that your money will be safe in a bank. This has been proven by the multiple hacking that have happened over the years.
There are hundreds of other cryptocurrencies, but Bitcoin was the first one that hit the market and was truly successful. earlier cryptocurrencies where the likes of B-money and Bit Gold, which Nakamoto has stated were part of the inspiration and concepts for Bitcoin. The rise of Bitcoin is what started the launch of the many other cryptocurrencies out there. Like all other cryptocurrencies, Bitcoin is not backed by banks. This means that it is not something that you can trade with like you can with money. There are some merchants that accept Bitcoin as a form of currency, but most do not. Granted there are more and more merchants that are choosing to accept Bitcoin as a form of currency. At this moment, you would not be able to make the switch to making all your transactions with Bitcoin as there are not enough Bitcoin accepting merchants to make this a viable option. In the future, cryptocurrency may very well take over the market and that will cause a huge shift in how we trade.
The draw towards Bitcoin is that it is unlike other forms of currency. It does not have the limitations that other, hard currencies have. If you want a rundown of what Bitcoin is and some of its characteristics then take a look at the list below:
  • There is a limited supply, unlike paper money which can just be printed whenever needed.
  • It is decentralized.
  • You can send Bitcoin anywhere in the world quickly and at cheap rates.
  • You can’t forge or falsify Bitcoin.
  • Everyone can see the transactions, so it is very transparent.
  • Bitcoin users remain anonymous. It is almost impossible for an outside user to discover the identity of the owner of a Bitcoin wallet.
Since its launch in 2009, Bitcoin has skyrocketed in value. There is no external source that determines its value. Instead, we as humans determine how much we think Bitcoin is worth. Now before you think that you can place any amount to a Bitcoin, let me explain further. The more people want something the higher the value of that item becomes. This is the same thing that happened with gold. When gold was first discovered, it was just a rock that had no value tacked onto it. As people wanted to own it, the value of gold increased. This increase in value just caused people to want it more which increased the value even more. This is the same thing that is happening with Bitcoin. This principle has been seen over and over again since the beginning where money was introduced into society. In order to understand where Bitcoin is going, we must understand the history of money, which will be discussed in the next section.
The Vision Behind Bitcoin
Currently, we have no choice but to trust the banks. They hold all the power when it comes to money and there is no guarantee that our money will be safe with them. We essentially just have faith that everything will be okay. Since the fall of the gold standard, everything surrounding money has been faith based. There is no set in stone way for governments to decide on the value of currency. This can change quite quickly and there is not really any protection from hyperinflation. We all just hope that the value of our money remains stable.
The first time people really realized that the system of using the bank to keep and control wealth had its flaws was in the Great Financial Crisis of 2007. Bitcoin was invented in 2008 as a response to this crisis. The reliance on banks as being an intermediary for all transactions and retention of money wasn’t looking as good as it used to. There have always been doubts on having the bank as a medium to which we deal with our money, but there has never been a good enough alternative solution. That is why the banks are still the way we do things today.
The main reason for the creation of Bitcoin was to cut out the middleman. When we want to send money to someone else, we have to use a bank as an intermediary. A transaction has to go from bank to bank in order for you to send or receive money. Using these intermediaries cause the users to incur hefty costs. There are transaction fees, fees for opening your bank account, and drawing money out of an ATM and using your card to swipe all incur fees. The banks have to employ people and keep their systems running and there are costs involved in taking the appropriate security measures to deal with money. These are the reasons behind the fees, but it does limit the end user. Often the user is restricted to the amount of money they can transact with. This has to do with the fees incurred by sending a certain amount of money or by the type of bank account they have. There are many bank account types that don’t allow transactions over certain amounts. We have gotten used to this way of doing things, but there are limitations to it.
When looking at costs incurred for transacting with a bank, most of the time the banks try to make this a standard fee regardless of the amount of money being transacted with. These standard fees are designed to cover the costs of the biggest transaction. This is so that the banks don’t lose money when people make big transactions. People also prefer to just have one streamlined amount than keeping track of all the various transaction fees that they might incur for different amounts of money. This means that you will be paying more than necessary for the majority of your transactions. Especially since most of us do smaller transactions quite frequently and bigger transactions only in limited amounts.
The bank fees that are incurred was definitely a big motivator for the development of Bitcoin, but not the only one. We already stated at the beginning of this section that trust is required when working with the bank. We have to trust that the bank will keep its word and not debase the currency it has in its power. We trust the banks to hold out money and keep it safe, but they use it to lend to other people and companies that need it. Most of the money that you think is in your bank account is not actually there. It is in someone else’s pocket who just used it for a transaction. When you go to draw out money you are actually taking out money that the bank has taken from someone else. This creates the fear that if something were to go drastically wrong with the economy or with the bank that you would not just be able to draw out your money and be on your way. You might actually just lose everything.
Banks are always looking to get a larger financial return. This is why they move the money around. They are investing and transferring it on our behalf. One of the biggest ways they make the money work for them is to lend it out to people who desperately need it. When someone wants to make a big purchase for a house or car, they usually don’t have enough money to buy it in cash. They have to borrow the money from somewhere. This place is usually a bank and the bank will charge them interest on the amount that they have lent to them. The bank gets more money from lending the money out and asking for it back with interest, than they ever would if they just left it in your account. If this is not done properly and there happens to be a case of unstable lending, it can lead to a market crash due to a large amount of losses. This can cause recessions and a whole host of terrible economic consequences. All this because everyone has to trust that the bank will be managing their money well.
The banks are required to keep some of their money in a reserve. The amount they keep is up to them and they use this money to pay out people that want to take out their money. Essentially, what happens when you deposit money into your bank account is that it goes into a large pool of money. From there the bank takes out some for the reserve and uses the other money for investing, lending, and whatever else they see fit to do with it. This is why you don’t really have the money available in your bank account. You will be able to draw it out, but you do not have full control of what happens to it when it is in the bank’s control.
When a country goes into a recession or there is a huge financial crisis, the government and the banks have to work on a solution to save the country’s economy. In 2008, the governments had to increase the amount of money they had in circulation. When this happens the value of the money goes down and this causes inflation. We have already discussed the hyperinflation in Zimbabwe and how that affected the country. Other countries like Argentina, Zambia, and Venezuela have also been subject to hyperinflation. These governments print more currency to pay off the debt. While this may work, it negatively impacts the people because they will essentially have less money than if they did before inflation (Mac, 2017). Let’s say that you have $20,000 saved up in your bank account. If you wanted to buy a car you could draw out this money and pay for the car cash. If inflation takes place the price of the car will go up in order to compensate for the inflation. Now, you still only have that $20,000 in your bank account, it does not change. The value of it just went down, so you would not be able to buy the car at this point. There is nothing you can do if inflation takes place, you just end up being less wealthy than you were the month, week, or even day before.
It’s not that banks are all bad and that they are willfully stealing your money. The process that is used in banking is fine for most transactions. There are definitely downsides, but a lot of the time, there isn’t any vast amounts of danger when you leave your money in a bank. There are definitely weaknesses in the trust-based model and that is what Bitcoin aims to fix.
Bitcoin was birthed to give the end user more control of their money. Inflation is controlled because there is no way to make more Bitcoin. The amount that is in circulation will be the same unless all the users agree to make more Bitcoin. It allows the end user to send money to another user without having to have their money go through a bank. Essentially the user has more control over their money. There is more security because of the open ledger that is available to everyone. Bitcoin is a currency that is transparent and does not hold any hidden or unnecessary fees.
The overall vision of Bitcoin was to give the user more control. The end user would not need their money to be controlled by a bank or subject to inflation caused by poor management. If you have Bitcoin, you control it and no one else. You don’t have to entrust it to anyone besides yourself. It is more secure overall.
Since we do not use physical currency for most of our transactions today, it does introduce an element of risk. Merchants have to hound their customers for the correct information, proof of payments can be faked, and there is a chance of the payments being able to be reversed. This is the other layer to the trust-based banking. We have to trust that we are being paid when there is very little solid proof. This is not just trust with the bank, but trust between merchant and customer.
When we used to use physical money, there was no need for trust. Someone would directly trade the physical money for an item. The transaction couldn’t be reversed and the merchant would receive the payment immediately. The proof of this payment was simply the money that they now had in their hands, risk of fraud and false payments was not even something to be concerned about. This system worked and is emulated in Bitcoin. The vision behind Bitcoin was to create a system of transacting that is as secure as trading physical money. It was also built with the principles of the gold standard to prevent inflation. It is all the good things of past systems of trading and using money, while all being on a digital platform. There would be no way for us to go back to trading with physical assets now that we have moved into a digital age. This is why the two had to be combined to create something that is viable for the world we live in today.
Understanding How Bitcoin Works
We already know that Bitcoin is digital currency and can be traded from peer-to-peer, without getting any intermediary involved. In most trading scenarios, there is a middleman. If you want to buy clothes you will have to go to the store instead of buying it straight from the factory. The store hikes up the prices by anywhere between 30%– 50% (or even more if they see fit) for their services. That means you will be paying much more than if you had just cut out the middleman. In this scenario, and most others, there is actually no way to cut out the middleman. The consumers have to foot the bill for the extra service provided by the middleman.
The same principle applies in banking. The banks are the middleman and anyone that uses their services has to pay a fee.
