If a person only kept cash and did not store their money in the bank, they could trade and not incur any fees. The money would also be given to the merchant immediately. All they do is take their money and give it to the merchant, who gives them their product. There is no middleman and therefore no additional fees or long waiting periods. However, in today’s world that is simply not possible. You have a bank account because no employer will send your money to you in cash. On top of that it is just not feasible to keep all of your savings in a plastic bag, under your mattress. This would restrict you from purchasing something and sending money overseas. In today’s world, that is a major limitation. Since you need the banks to send money to other countries they have worked out deals with banks from various locations and you usually have to pay very high fees in order to send your cash. It is frustrating that it costs so much just to pay for something to send money to other locations.
Bitcoin came along to solve this problem and remove the banks from the equation. There is no middleman involved, which means that you can send your currency through to other users in no time at all and you don’t have all the additional bank charges. There are fees involved with Bitcoin transactions, but they work differently to that of banks and you don’t have to pay any money to have a Bitcoin account. The fees associated with transactions do not have anything to do with where the receiver of the Bitcoin is located. You can send money internationally at the same fee as sending money to someone who is standing right next to you.
That’s not the only problem with the banks. When you sign up for a bank account, you give them a lot of personal information. If a bank is hacked, which happens quite often, all of your personal information is up for grabs. With Bitcoin you do not have to give in any personal information and all transactions are anonymous. One other problem that comes with banks is that they have the right to block or freeze your account without first consulting you. This gives them a lot of control over money that isn’t theirs. All of this is remedied in the Bitcoin framework. It was designed as a currency that would not allow the banks to have so much control over everyone’s money.
The only way to solve all of this was to create a currency that did not need a central authority to regulate it. It gives full control to the users and no one else. There are three essential components to Bitcoin and how it works. Understanding each one of these will give you a clear picture of what Bitcoin is and how it works. These three concepts are cryptography, supply and demand, and decentralized networks. You have heard some of these terms in this book already and you will hear these terms again. They form the base framework in which Bitcoin can function.
Cryptography
The basic definition of cryptography is turning normal text into unintelligible text and then back again. The purpose of this is so that the data can be stored and sent to the relevant people without it being read by parties that are not involved in the communication. It protects against theft, fraud, and from the data being altered in any way. This process can also be used to authenticate users so that the wrong person does not gain access to any sensitive information (The Economic Times, n.d.).
Cryptography provides protection and anonymity for users across the board. It is basically like a digital signature that cannot be forged. The sender encrypts the message, or in this case Bitcoin, by using a specific key or algorithm. When the receiver gets it they have to decrypt it so that they can see the message or receive the Bitcoin. Now when it comes to Bitcoin you don’t have to decrypt it yourself, it is done for you. It is just like how some messaging apps are end-to-end encrypted. The people on the sending and receiving ends aren’t doing much besides typing out their messages. The software encrypts the information so that only the parties involved in the conversation can see the message (Seth, 2020).
Bitcoin utilizes cryptography though blockchain. We will discuss that in the nest section, so that you can get a good idea of what blockchain is and how it works.
Supply and Demand
We spoke about the gold standard earlier on in the book. If you remember, we said that people decided on the price of gold because they put the value on it. Without people thinking it was valuable it would just be another metal. There is nothing intrinsically special about it that would make it valuable. It is only as valuable as the number of people who want to get their hands on it and the supply of it available. If a lot of gold was found then, because there is a lot in supply, the price of gold will drop. As soon as it becomes scarce again, the price of gold would go up, because now the commodity is rarer and in high demand.
Bitcoin was designed to mimic the gold standard. There are only 21 million Bitcoins to be mined. Once they are gone, they are gone forever. Not all the available Bitcoins are in circulation at this moment. There are still a few million waiting to be mined. At the end of 2020, there were still just over two million waiting to be mined. Mining is much slower than it was when Bitcoin was first launched. This was done on purpose so that not all of the Bitcoins would be mined at a fast pace. It looks like the last remaining Bitcoin will only be able to be mined in 2140 so there is still a lot of time until that happens.
The slow pace of Bitcoin mining will result in a price rise, because the addition of new Bitcoins is much lower than the demand for them. Bitcoin is growing in popularity so people are eager to get their hands on some. Once all the Bitcoins have been mined the only thing that will determine the price of Bitcoin in the market is whether or not people see it as being a practical solution for currency and therefore resulting in a demand for it. If it does not meet the legal standards or is just not a viable solution the price will drop due to people no longer having a purpose for it. This is yet to be seen and we will probably have to wait a few more years before we can get a good sense of what the future holds for Bitcoin and cryptocurrency as a whole (Bloomenthal, 2021).
Decentralized Networks
Most networks are centralized, this just means that there is one place where all the data is stored. Let’s take Facebook for example. It has a centralized network. All the information that comes from Facebook and that people put into Facebook when they are setting up their profiles is all stored in one place. If the Facebook server was to crash then Facebook would be down and no one would be able to login until the issue was resolved. Out of the central servers, it attaches itself to millions of different computers, phones, and tablets. All information comes from one place and goes back to that same place. Because it is set up like this, Facebook can regulate what people can see on their feeds, they can sensor content, and they can basically control everything that goes on in the Facebook world.
You can think of centralization as a bee colony. The beehive would be the main server that everything runs from. While each bee has its own function and is technically separate from the hive it is still attached in some way. The honey bee can go out to different flowers and drop off pollen wherever it needs to, but it will always come back to the beehive. There is nothing that goes on with the bees that the queen does not control. If the beehive and the queen was destroyed then the bees would not have a function. This is because the bees get their instruction from the queen and their whole lives are centered around coming back to the hive for instruction.
Even though information is moved throughout the smaller devices, it is not controlled by it. We can see the buttons, pictures, and statuses, but we do not have control over it. Even if we were to type out a status on our Facebook page, the Facebook head office could still take it down and have full control of the page. If they want to block a user they can and they can define what the community guidelines are. You may have an account with Facebook but you do not have full control over it.
Centralized networks are definitely needed. There are things that need to be regulated. If someone was to post something extremely inappropriate on a public platform, the platform should have a right to take it down if it goes against what the platform stands for. The users will then have to decide if they agree with this or not. If they do not they can move to a different platform that has guidelines that they agree with. The problem that many people have with centralized networks is when it pertains to their money. Allowing someone else to have full control and power over money has become a big problem. It is not that the banks have necessarily done anything bad, but the risk is quite high especially when it comes to people’s money.
A decentralized network is the opposite. Instead of having a central place where everything is processed, there are multiple machines to which the workload is distributed. Decentralization was not always possible because technology was limited. Since computers and laptops have a lot more power and capabilities on them than they used to, it is possible to distribute the workload over multiple devices that are not in the same area. Most laptops and home computers have far greater capabilities than the average user knows what to do with (N-able, 2018).
When something is decentralized, the possibility of it going offline is so small. There are so many machines that are running the program that switching them all off would not be a possibility. It is also much harder to hack a system that is decentralized because not all the information is in one place. There is no one entity that has control over everything so you do not have to trust the entity in order to keep your information or money safe. It gives you more control and security. Every user has the same information, the same access, and ultimately every user controls their own piece of the information.
Decentralization should not be confused with a distributed network. A distributed network also has various machines in various places, but is still controlled by a single entity. In layman’s terms, decentralization means that every single person is their own server. There are millions of devices that are linked together and from here the system works. Each person only has control over their portion of it. If you switch off your network, then it will have no effect on the other people.
Decentralization means that there is complete transparency because every person sees the exact same thing. With the Facebook example, we only see what they allow us to see. We do not know or have any control of what is happening in the back end. With Bitcoin everyone can see everything. Even if you do not fully understand what is going on you still have the option to see everything and use the functions available (BitDegree, 2020).
Common Questions Surrounding Bitcoin
There are many questions that surround Bitcoin and the use of it. It is good to get a better understanding of Bitcoin by answering these questions. Below are the most commonly asked questions that people are asking right now.
Where Can You Use Bitcoin?
The big question surrounding Bitcoin is, “Where can you spend it?” When someone buys into a currency, the expectation is that they can spend it on real-world items. Now, it has taken some time to get to this point, but there are quite a few retailers and companies that accept Bitcoin. Many more will be joining the movement as Bitcoin grows in popularity.
There is something to be said about the earlier adopters of using Bitcoin as a medium of payment. These companies are testing the status quo and could reap a lot of benefits by leading the way. Many companies are skeptical because Bitcoin is not something that has been tested over a long period of time. However, the expectation is that in a few years there will be many more companies that will jump on the Bitcoin bandwagon.
If you have some Bitcoin that you would like to spend, you can do so at the following places:
  • Microsoft: You can purchase from the online Xbox store using Bitcoin. This has been the case since 2014. Microsoft founder, Bill Gates, has been known to be a huge proponent of Bitcoin.
  • Starbucks: The coffee giant accepts payment through an app called Bakkt. All you have to do is download the app and you can use Bitcoin to make payments in Starbucks.
  • Overstock: This is a retailer that mainly stocks furniture and other household items. You can actually use many different types of cryptocurrency on this online store.
  • Home Depot: I’m sure this store needs no introduction. It is the largest hardware store chain in America. Most people will have one not far from them. You can use Bitcoin to purchase anything in the store.
  • Whole Foods: You would be able to pay for your groceries using Bitcoin if you use the app Spend. Once it has been downloaded, you can pay instore using it. There are actually many other retailers that you can buy from when you use the app, including Baskin Robbins and Jamba Juice.
  • Newegg: This company sells computer hardware. You can actually buy cryptocurrency mining software from here as well.
  • AT&T and Virgin Mobile: These cell phone carriers and cellular network providers have started supporting Bitcoin payments. These are the first two in the field that have done so. This might pave the way for other cellular companies to do the same.
  • Expedia: On this website you can purchase and book your flights. People who travel a lot for either business or pleasure will appreciate this one.
  • Twitch: This is a platform that allows users to stream activities that relate to gaming. Many people use it to stream their live video games. You can use Bitcoin to purchase subscriptions on the platform.
  • Subway: This major food chain accepts Bitcoin at some of their stores. The first store to accept a Bitcoin payment was actually in Russia. Since this option is not available at every store, you will have to keep your eye out for one that does. There will mostly likely be more stores that accept Bitcoin in the near future.
  • Tesla: This is a very recent addition to the list of places you can buy using Bitcoin. It has something to do with the massive investment the founder of the company, Elon Musk, made into the cryptocurrency. You can now buy the electric car directly from Tesla and pay in Bitcoin. The website will show you the price in U.S. dollars and the Bitcoin equivalent. This introduction might trigger many other companies to move to this direction since many people have faith in Musk’s directions and often follow suit. Keep your eye out for other companies jumping on the bandwagon.
As you can see, there are plenty of places where you can spend your Bitcoin. You may not be able to may not be able to completely swear off regular currency for a move to cryptocurrency, but you can make a lot of your purchases using Bitcoin. If you really try, a majority of what you buy can be bought using Bitcoin. If you really want to use your Bitcoin to purchase things from stores that do not offer this option, you can use Purse.io. This is an app that allows you to choose a product or site you want to buy from and then it links you to someone who has points or a gift card there. The person will buy the product for you and you will pay them in Bitcoin through the app. It is a very roundabout way to get your item, but it is making it possible to shop for various items using Bitcoin.
There might be other companies and stores where you can spend your Bitcoin, these are just some of the major ones. Doing a bit more personal research will lead you to finding a whole lot more places to spend your accumulated Bitcoin (Tuwiner, 2021).
How Do Bitcoin Transaction Fees Work?
When someone makes a transaction with Bitcoin it is added to the public ledger. Once it has been added to this ledger the transaction is confirmed and the Bitcoin is transferred from one party to another. Bitcoin miners add these transactions to the blockchain and they get compensated for it. They will have to do this with a cluster of transactions that cannot exceed 1 MB, this cluster is called a block.
Since a block can only contain a cluster of transactions that cannot exceed 1 MB, there can only be a certain number of transactions included in the block. When there are lots of people trying to make a transaction, it means that there are more transactions that there is space in the block. The miners choose which transactions should be included in the block, naturally the ones with the highest fees get chosen first so that they will get compensated more. As a user you can compete to get your transaction into a block by increasing the fees for it. Once the market has reached its maximum equilibrium fee and the miners will work through all the transactions in order. Once the transaction traffic has slowed down, the transaction fees will come back down because people will no longer be competing to get their transaction through first.
Since the block can only be 1 MB in size, the size of the transaction does matter. Far fewer large transactions can be added into a block than smaller transactions. Miners generally prefer to add in the smaller transactions into the block because they will be compensated more for it. At the beginning of this process all transactions can have the same fees. Then the users who have larger transactions waiting for approval will have to increase their fees in order for the miners to include them in the next block.
There is no set fee for transactions on Bitcoin, it all depends on the congestion caused by the number of transactions waiting for approval. It is also difficult to predict the size of the transaction. There are fee calculators available online on certain websites. This can give you an estimate of how much you will need to pay in fees should you want your transaction to get through quickly.
As you can see the size of your transaction does not really matter. What matters is the time in which you make a transaction. If there is no congestion then you will be able to pay a lower fee. If there are lots of people wanting to get their transactions through then the fees will be higher. There aren’t any set fees for transactions made with Bitcoin (Blockchain Support Center, n.d.).
Is It Legal to Use Bitcoin?
The simple answer is yes, but it also depends on your country and the activity of the user. Different countries have different regulations when it comes to cryptocurrencies, so you will need to check your local government’s stance on this matter. Regardless of which country you are in, Bitcoin is not legally acceptable as a replacement currency for the country’s legal currency.
Here is a list of some of the countries where Bitcoin is legal. There will be different laws governing the use of Bitcoin so make sure you know what you are allowed to do and what is restricted in each of these countries. This is not a comprehensive list so do some research if your country is not listed here:
  • The United States of America
  • Canada
  • The European Union
  • Japan
  • The United Kingdom
  • South Africa
  • Ukraine
Here is the list of countries of countries where Bitcoin is illegal. The reasoning for this will different for each country, but most see it as a threat to their current currency or are weary of its volatility and the fact that it is decentralized. You will have to check to what degree Bitcoin is banned in the country in which you live. The legality of Bitcoin can change at any time so keep up to date with the laws:
  • China
  • Russia
  • Vietnam
  • Bolivia
  • Ecuador
  • Columbia
  • Saudi Arabia
  • Afghanistan
  • India
Certain countries allow Bitcoin with some restrictions, while some are still unclear about whether they allow it or not. The bottom line is that you will have to check out your country’s stance on Bitcoin before you move forward with it. Even if your country is on the list of the countries that do allow Bitcoin, you should still find out to what extent and if there are any restrictions or regulations. The rules and laws of this can change at any point so it is important to stay up to date with what is going on in your country and state with regards to this (Bajpai, 2019).
Can Bitcoin Be Regulated?
The nature of Bitcoin and any other cryptocurrency is that it is not regulated by any government or any legal entity. It is essentially freewheeling and cannot be subject to any rules or regulation made by a government besides the ones that prohibit its use in their country.
Most governments do expect people who cash out their assets in the country’s currency to pay cash on it. If you use your Bitcoin as a medium to purchase items with, then there should not be tax applied to that. It is not that governments don’t want citizens to pay tax on cryptocurrencies, there is just no system for it yet. It is not possible to assess how much cryptocurrency tax an individual would be liable for until a form of software is developed to do this. This software would need to be pretty sophisticated and has not yet been developed.
Most governing bodies do want to regulate Bitcoin and other cryptocurrencies, but it is not a problem that has a solution at the moment. There are people out there that are working to find a solution to this, but there hasn’t been much news of progress in this area. There are various Acts and laws that have been put into place with regards to cryptocurrency, but very few in terms of regulations have been accepted or implemented. Eventually regulation of cryptocurrencies will be a must, as most countries are working hard at this, but for now it is limited (Sharma, 2020b).
Countries can assign rights and regulations to how they want Bitcoin to be used in their own country. This is completely within their control. It is just like any other currency that can be used to purchase items. If a country does not want their people to be able to use Bitcoin for a specific purpose they can impose laws and regulations to prevent this from happening. It is just the internal regulation of Bitcoin that governments have no control over.
If a specific person or entity wanted to be able to regulate transactions and block or reverse certain transactions, they could only do so by investing in Bitcoin mining software and work to control half of the computing power of this platform. The problem with this is that there really is no guarantee that they would be able to retain this power as they would have to continuously invest more than other miners around the world. The Bitcoin software is designed to be community run, so if there is a change in protocol that is put forward, it has to be agreed upon by almost all the users. It is highly unlikely that everyone who uses Bitcoin would want to give regulatory power to one local authority (Bitcoin, n.d.).
How Is the Price of Bitcoin Determined?
The price of Bitcoin is constantly changing, the reason for this is because the price is determined by supply and demand. When demand is high the price is driven up and when the demand is low the price is low. There will only ever be 21 million Bitcoins in the world. Not all of them have been mined, there are just over 18 million Bitcoins that have been mined. When the remaining Bitcoins have been mined the world’s supply of Bitcoin would be tapped out and no more can be created, this is why it is so similar to gold. All 21 million Bitcoin will be circulated and used as is with no prospect for more. The price of each coin will fluctuate depending on demand.
Since Bitcoin is not being used by every person, and there is only a small market of people that are using it, it can be quite volatile. This means that it does not take a lot for the price to fluctuate. In the future the price might stabilize, but that is still to be seen as there are factors to consider.
Bitcoins also risk becoming worthless, just like every other currency has the same risk. We have already seen currencies become worthless, the most recent of this being the Zimbabwean dollar. Granted this was caused by hyperinflation, which is impossible to happen to Bitcoin because more cannot be made. However, there is always a possibility for some sort of technical failure and even competition from other currencies that might arise. There really is no guarantee that Bitcoin will be around forever. This is just the rule when it comes to any currency. We can’t maintain that any kind of currency will be in use indefinitely. There is always a risk of hard times or some sort of unexpected problem or failure.
So far Bitcoin has proven to be reliable, but it has not been around for a very long time. There might be a problem that could arise that could turn Bitcoin on its head. Investors should always be aware of the risk involved. With all of that being said there is no current evidence to suggest that Bitcoin will lose its value in the foreseeable future. There is actually a lot of potential for it to grow and increase in price. It is just good to be aware of the potential risks even if there aren’t any red flags at the moment (Bitcoin, n.d.).
Is Bitcoin Being Used for Illegal Activities?
The short answer is yes, it can be used for illegal activities, but so can any other form of currency. Bitcoin is a form of currency that can be used to buy various items. Unfortunately, this does include illegal items. People buy illegal items, so this is just the reality of money and the world we live in. The current banking and monetary systems far supersede Bitcoin in terms of illegal use.
The regular money that is in circulation today can be counterfeited and used for illegal purposes in that capacity, where there is no risk of that happening with Bitcoin. Bitcoin actually prevents financial crime much more than regular money does. Each user is in complete control of all their transactions and cannot review any charges that have not been approved, which is what happens in the case of credit card fraud. Theft and loss are also prevented with Bitcoin, which adds to the financial safety of the system.
When people ask this question about illegal activities, their minds go to buying illegal items. However, there are many other illegal activities out there. In today’s world there is a lot of financial crime. There are most likely more people out there that try and commit financial crimes that are trying to buy illegal items. It is actually very unwise to use Bitcoin for illegal purposes because it can be tracked using the blockchain technology. The public ledger is put in place so that everyone can view the transactions being made. Criminals can be tracked as their transactions are always going to show up on the chain.
If there were any sorts of illegal activities going on and the people behind this are using Bitcoin, criminal investigations can be undertaken. It is really not likely that Bitcoin would stop and criminal investigations should it be necessary (Bitcoin, n.d.).
Has Bitcoin Ever Been Hacked?
There has never been a hack in the Bitcoin system. There have, however, been a few cases of theft and security breaches that have happened on diverse exchanges. These have been dealt with and the system has been improved as a result of it. With new systems, it is almost impossible to not have any problems. As they arise, the developers are able to see the blind spots and work on those to prevent these types of things in the future.
The things that have happened do not mean that there is a flaw with Bitcoin itself. We wouldn’t say that the dollar has been compromised if it has been stolen out from the teller at the bank. These scenarios are unfortunate, but Bitcoin, itself, has never been hacked or compromised. New security systems have been developed to prevent any future incidents.
Can Bitcoins Get Lost?
It is possible to lose a Bitcoin. However, this is not the traditional way of losing something. Since everything is digital, the only way you can lose your Bitcoin is if you lose your Bitcoin wallet. Since nobody will be able to find the private keys to the Bitcoin it will be lost forever. This means that it will just not be circulated anymore. It will remain in the blockchain, but it can’t be used so it will remain dormant.
This will most likely drive up the price of the remaining Bitcoins in order to compensate for the loss. There will be less Bitcoins in circulation, and because of supply and demand, this is the result. It is not a serious thing, but the person who has lost their Bitcoin will be at a loss. This is the same as someone losing their real wallet that is filled with cash. There is not much that can be done about it. If you misplace the wallet, then it's gone for good (Bitcoin, n.d.).
What Are Bitcoin Wallets?
A Bitcoin wallet is not a physical wallet, but a software that you can store your Bitcoin in. To be technical, Bitcoin is not actually stored in any place. When you have Bitcoin, you will get a key that allows you to open it and transact with it. If you have a balance in your Bitcoin wallet you will have a specific key, which is a series of numbers, that will correspond with your wallet. A Bitcoin wallet just facilitates the receiving and sending of the Bitcoin between different users.
If you want to trade with your Bitcoin, you will need to set up a Bitcoin wallet. What you would think a real wallet does, is exactly what a regular wallet does but in a digital sense. All the relevant information will be stored in this wallet. There are various types of wallets you can use and they are desktop, mobile, web, and hardware wallets. They each are slightly different from each other and you will have to decide which ones you would want to suit your needs. There are many brands for each kind of wallet, so you will be spoilt for choice. Most of these wallets are free, with the exception of hardware wallets. See the quick destruction of each of them below:
  • Desktop wallets: This is installed on your desktop and is an address where other users can send Bitcoins to you. You can store a private key in the wallet and have complete control over your wallet.
  • Mobile wallets: They perform the exact same function as a desktop wallet but can be carried around for paying in store. You can use touch-to-pay and QR codes for transactions if the merchants accept Bitcoin. Most of them are iOS and Android compatible.
  • Web wallets: This type of wallet allows you to access your Bitcoin from anywhere, as long as you have an internet connection. You can swap between your computer and phone, because all you need to do is launch your browser and go to the relevant web address. You should choose your web wallet carefully and go with something that has been proven to be reliable. It will store your private key on the web so this is the reason you need to make sure that one you choose is secure.
  • Hardware wallets: If you are looking for a very secure Bitcoin wallet then this is the right choice for you. The reason they are so secure is that they are a physical piece of equipment that must be plugged into a computer or something with a USB port. Once you are done you can disconnect the wallet from your device and place it somewhere safe. This makes the hardware wallet almost immune to getting a virus. The price of these will vary depending on which you get, but you can expect to spend somewhere around $100–$200 for one.
Hackers can target Bitcoin wallets if they have a high value. Make sure to keep them safe. Some people choose to store their Bitcoin offline to increase the security. Regardless of which one you choose, they all perform the same function. The user is expected to choose the one that suits them the best (Frankenfield, 2020b).
What Are the Advantages and Disadvantages of Using Bitcoin?
Like with everything in life there are advantages and disadvantages to Bitcoin. It is helpful to know what they are so that the user has a full idea of what to expect when they sign up for Bitcoin. See the list of advantages and disadvantages below (Investerest, 2019):
Advantages:
  • It is completely transparent: Since it is run on blockchain technology, it makes it possible to see all the transactions as they happen. Nothing will be hidden and all information is available to all users.
  • Ease of overseas transfers: Bitcoin can be transferred to any account, anywhere in the world. There will be no extra fee attached to overseas payments and it does not take a longer time to get to them. This is because there is no middleman and Bitcoin is a strictly peer-to-peer system.
  • Decentralized: Bitcoin does operate with the banks or governments of any country. This means that people would be able to get their payments faster as there is no middleman.
  • Low risk and privacy: All personal information is hidden from the public. When someone makes a payment, any personal information regarding the identity of the people involved is not able to be seen on the blockchain. The blockchain also detects any false payments and stops them immediately, which means there is a much lower risk of fraud. Any false payments cannot be covered up because everything is available to see on the ledger.
  • It’s fast: Banks and financial institutions can take days to clear a transaction. With Bitcoin your transaction can be cleared and sent within minutes.
Disadvantages:
  • Legal limits: Governments have restricted or prohibited the sale and use of Bitcoin in certain countries. It is well within the countries rights to prohibit it, but it does make it difficult to use Bitcoin in some countries.
  • It’s volatile: The price of Bitcoin is constantly going up and down. This volatility causes many merchants to not accept it as a mode of payment. Unpredictability poses a risk for those that need to receive payment.
  • Loss of keys: Bitcoin wallets are very easy to use with their public and private keys. Often one of these keys, or even both of them, are lost and that means the user cannot gain access to their Bitcoin. There is an estimated 2.8–3.8 million Bitcoins that have been lost and are no longer in circulation. Since there are only 21 million Bitcoins that will ever be created, it makes it a bit difficult that so many of them are being lost.
  • Irreversible transactions: The irreversibility of transactions is a positive thing when it comes to people trying to action false transactions. However, the negative to this is that if a user makes a mistake or there is a faulty transaction the transaction will still be irreversible. This poses a problem for those who need to get their Bitcoin back. It is pretty much impossible unless the receiver agrees to send it back to them, which will not always be the case.
  • Varying fees: The way fees work has already been described in a previous section. It does get difficult to predict fees as they are based on the congestion of transactions at any given time. In many cases, you will not know how much the fees will cost you until you are ready to make the transaction.

Chapter 6:
Bitcoin Mining
Bitcoin mining can be quite a lucrative operation if you do it properly. There are thousands of people out there that mine Bitcoin so that they can make a profit. It is quite interesting the way it works and incentivizes people to mine these coins. Bitcoin mining is an essential part of the Bitcoin framework. Knowing how mining works and what goes on behind the scenes is quite important if you want to get involved in the Bitcoin world.
What Exactly Is It?
The best way to describe Bitcoin mining is to use regular gold mining as an example. Essentially, this is like the new gold rush. Lots of people are trying to mine Bitcoin so that they can be financially compensated for it. What would happen back in the days of gold mining is that many people would go out and mine for gold out of their own will, and then once they found the gold they would sell it for financial compensation. The same thing is happening with Bitcoin mining. The miners do it out of their own will in order to get some sort of financial compensation for it.
When Bitcoin is mined, the mined Bitcoin is moved into circulation for use. The miners then get paid an amount in Bitcoin as well. This is how new Bitcoin keeps entering circulation. This is not the only function it performs as it also maintains and is critical in the development of the blockchain ledger. This is done by the use of very sophisticated computers. These computers solve complex math problems that result in the mining of Bitcoin (Hong, 2021).
This mining is not for everyone since it is painstaking. It takes a lot of effort and investment from those who want to mine. The other thing is that it is not consistently rewarding. Only if the person mines successfully will they get rewarded. Again, this can be brought back to the gold mine example. People could pan for gold for hours, even days and not find anything. Efforts would only be rewarded if they did find gold.
Even with all of this taken into account many people are still attracted to the idea of mining because the reward is crypto tokens. Miners get access to tokens that other people will not and it is a different way to make cryptocurrency. Many people who start mining are technologically inclined, which is why they are drawn to Bitcoin mining.
There is also another benefit to Bitcoin mining, aside from the chance for compensation. Bitcoin is a system that is completely run by the people who use it. In order for a new idea or suggestion to be implemented, it needs to be agreed upon by the people who use the platform. When you are a miner you have the power to vote on anything that may arise. Miners have some influence over the chances that happen to the Bitcoin platform and system. This is another draw to becoming a miner. People love to have power and this is a good way to have your say when the time comes.
What Is Needed to Mine Bitcoin
If someone is interested in Bitcoin mining, then they need to arm themselves with the right tools for the job. Mining is not a small task and you won’t be able to use any computer or laptop to do it. When Bitcoin had first started out, people could use smaller machines to mine. Now you will need a Bitcoin mining rig to get started on the process. To be specific, you need an ASIC (Application-Specific Integrated Circuit chips) Bitcoin mining rig.
This machine does not come cheap, and you will need the space to store it. While it is running, it is quite noisy, so people who have small apartments probably won’t get away with running this for long periods of time. The most powerful of its kind are extremely fast and powerful. They can take an average of 1,012 tries to solve a block. This might seem like a lot of attempts, but compared to any other machine this is a super speed. There aren’t many machines that can actually work this fast, so it is pretty heavy duty. (Grygaříková, 2018).
Choosing the right rig is important so anyone who is seriously interested in mining Bitcoin will need to spend some time researching and picking the right one. This is not a decision to be made on the fly. The most important things to consider are the consumption of electricity, the price, and the hash rate. The hash rate is the performance of the machine because it indicates the amount of times the machine has to try to solve the mathematical problem before it succeeds.
A big mistake that people make is to pick a cheaper option and overlook power consumption. This is a powerful machine and some of them can eat up a lot of electricity. If this is the case, then you will be exceeding the amount you initially saved by paying for your electricity bills. Always look at reviews before purchasing this rig. These machines are not easy to get because they are so in demand at the moment. Other things that need to be taken into account are where you will put the mining rig and the fees you will be paying to join a mining pool, this will be discussed later on in this section. With regards to the placement of the rig, it is advisable for it to be placed in a professional data center. This does cost you some money, but you will have peace of mind that the area is cool enough and you won’t be bothered by the constant noise emanating from it as it is being mined.
The next thing that is needed to mine Bitcoin is a Bitcoin wallet. This is for obvious reasons as you need to have some place to put your Bitcoins once they have been mined. There is a lot of information about Bitcoin wallets and which ones are appropriate for different situations in the next chapter, so we will just leave this here.
There are a few other things that you need, but they are not as technical as the rig. For this reason you will find the other items in the list below. A better explanation of what they do and why you need them will be given when we discuss how to mine Bitcoin (BitDegree, 2021a):
  • An application to mine
  • Ventilation equipment—Fans and ducting if you are using a professional data center
  • A pool membership
  • Power Splitters
  • Power supply units, especially if you have a very big rig
  • Ethernet cable
  • Internet connection
  • Computer that is able to browse the internet
Once you have all of this you can start mining your Bitcoin. We will discuss the specifics of this in the next section.
How to Do It
Mining gets more and more difficult as time goes on. The way Bitcoin is designed is so that miners will not be able to mine at the same pace for extended periods of time. In the same way that gold mining got more difficult as the resources were depleted, Bitcoin follows that trend. As time goes on you will find it more difficult to get a smaller amount of Bitcoin.
Bitcoin mining is not just for getting new Bitcoins into circulation. Miners are used to verify transactions to help solve the double spending problem. Double spending is a risk with any kind of digital currency because there is not anything physical being transacted with. If there aren’t any measures put in place then someone could easily spend the same Bitcoin over and over again to buy different items. In the physical world, when you have a $5 bill you can exchange that bill for a Big Mac. Once you give the money over to the cashier there is no chance of you spending it on anything else. You can’t go ahead and take that $5 and buy chicken wings from the restaurant right next-door. With digital currency, someone could make a copy of the digital coin and send someone the fake one while keeping the original one with them (Hong, 2021).
A Bitcoin miner checks to make sure that no one is trying to use the same Bitcoin twice. In the physical world, you could look at the serial numbers of the $5 bill to see if it has been used before. This is basically the same principle to how the miners stop the double spending problem.
The miner has to mine 1 MB worth of data in order to be eligible to get paid out. The 1 MB of data is known as a block. The amount of transactions that can be verified in a block can be as little as one or more than a thousand. Different transactions take up a different amount of space so this is the reason for that. Now take note of the fact that a miner will only be eligible to be compensated if they mine a block of data. This means that there is no guarantee that they will be paid out for their work.
In order to actually be compensated for mining, you have to mine the 1 MB of data and you need to be the first person to reach the right answer to the numeric problem. Bitcoin runs on mathematical problems. New Bitcoin can only be released when these mathematical problems have been solved. There are lots of miners working on solving these problems so you need to be the first to get the exact right number or get the closest. This is pure luck. There is nothing you can do to make sure that you will be the first one to solve the problem. Once you have met both of these criteria you can expect to be compensated for your efforts.
This math problem would be enough to make people not want to mine Bitcoin at all. The good news is that the miner does not actually have to sit down and try and solve the math problem using advanced mathematical skills or extreme computational power. Instead you need to be patient because the whole thing is just a bunch of guesswork and technically the person does not have to put in that much effort if they have the right equipment, which we spoke about in the previous section.
The math problem that needs to be solved is a 64-digit, hexadecimal number. This means that there are over a trillion different combinations that could be the right one. The miners are trying to be the first ones to get as close to that number. This process is referred to as a hash, which was discussed in a previous chapter on blockchain. In order to be a successful miner, you need to have a very high hash rate. This requires the miners to have a lot of computing power to support the process. Just because you have this fast computer does not mean that you will be guaranteed to get compensated for your Bitcoin. It is still up to luck, it’s just that the people with the fastest computers will have a higher chance of solving the problem. There is a chance that someone with a very low hash rate could get lucky and solve the problem well before the rest of the miners. This doesn’t happen often, but is a possibility.
Joining a Mining Pool
Since it is much harder to mine Bitcoin now, third parties and groups have decided to operate in mining pools. In a mining pool, you are not mining on your own, but with a group of people. The positive of this is that the group has a much higher chance of mining Bitcoin than if someone was just to go at it on their own. The negative is that, if you are the one who mines the Bitcoin successfully, you will have to share your reward with all the miners in the pool, but the same will go for anyone else in the pool who ends up mining successfully.
Joining a mining pool is definitely the easiest and most reliable way to make sure that your mining efforts are actually profitable. Usually you will receive small payments of Bitcoin on a regular basis because there is a large number of people to share with, and the pool will most likely be mining successfully every so often. The income that you are receiving might be lower but is regular. There are more people who prefer this so that they don’t have to take the risk of buying all the mining equipment and having a very low chance of getting rewarded for it (Grygaříková, 2018).
If you want to join a mining pool, it helps if you do some research on it. Not all mining pools are exactly the same, so there are a few things that you would have to consider. The first thing you will need to consider is the size of the mining pool. Some pools are really large and others are much smaller. There are positives and negatives to both of these. The positive is that the more people in the pool, the higher chance of you getting a reward. More people working on it together gives everyone a better chance of being compensated for their efforts. The negative to a larger pool is that your compensation will be lower because it needs to be split among many more people. Usually novice miners should go for a larger pool as they will be compensated quite regularly and would not have to utilize that much electricity. It is generally the cheaper option that yields a good payoff to beginner miners.
The next thing that you will need to consider is the minimum payment you will be able to withdraw from the pool. Pools usually stipulate at which point you are allowed to take out the Bitcoin that you have earned. It is best to pick a pool that has a pretty low minimum payment. This means that you will be able to take out regular payments even if it is a very small amount of Bitcoin. Low minimum payment pools are generally safer because there is a lower chance of something bad happening. It is the worst feeling to wait until you have a larger amount of Bitcoin so that you can take it out and then the pool suddenly disappears or something bad happens in the world of crypto that results in a price drop of Bitcoin. Taking out your Bitcoin whenever you can is definitely the safer option so that you know you have it with you should anything happen.
Joining a pool is not completely free, so the next thing that you will have to consider before joining one is the fees that you will incur. These fees are not upfront when you join a pool, rather you have to pay a percentage fee on the Bitcoin you mine. This fee is taken out automatically before you get your reward. Obviously, the lower the fee the better for you. You will be able to retain most of your rewards if the fee is quite low. Usually the fees to join a mining pool is around 1%–3%, depending on the pool. There are some mining pools that have no joining fees in order to join, but these have not been around long enough to assess the reliability of them. A new miner should go for the pools that have fees just because they are more reputable. Just be sure to find one that has a low percentage fee (BitDegree, 2021a).
Mine on Your Own
Mining alone is possible and there are some people that choose to go with this option. When beginners get into mining, they often think it is the best option because then they don’t have to pay a fee or share the rewards when they happen to mine successfully. While that may be tempting, it is not the best option for most people. You won’t have to share your reward with others, but it also means that you won’t get to share the rewards from the other miners either. A mining pool with lots of people will have a lower amount that can be withdrawn, but it is consistent and in the long run you might end up making more Bitcoin in a pool than on your own.
As a solo miner you will only get paid out if you are the one that solves the hash. If you don’t, then you will get no reward. The chances of a solo miner solving a hash is pretty low because you are competing with every pool and every solo miner in the Bitcoin mining world. That is a lot of competition for one person. It is not that it is impossible for you to solve the hash first, the chances are just extremely low. Even if you have a lot of commuting power that is stronger than all the solo miners out there, you will definitely not have more computing power than the mining pools.
The people who could be successful at solo mining operations are those who are already very rich. A millionaire could fund a huge mining operation and then have a good chance of reaping the rewards. You would need to buy hundreds of mining rigs to have a good chance of mining Bitcoin successfully. Then take into account the electricity and the fact that you will probably need to store all of those mining rigs in a warehouse with sufficient cooling to prevent the rigs from overheating. These rigs make quite a bit of noise when they are working so anyone who has this size of an operation would not be able to keep it in their house if they value their sleep.
Cloud Mining
With cloud mining you don’t do any of the work, but contract yourself to a company that has all the equipment. The company buys all the necessary machines and takes care of them and you pay them to mine for you. This way you don’t have to worry about buying any equipment or maintaining. While this sounds great and far less work than regular Bitcoin mining, there are some things to consider if you want to get into cloud mining.
The reason there are cloud mining companies and the owners don’t just min for themselves is because they want to have a guaranteed profit coming from the investment they made. Regardless if the price of Bitcoin drops dramatically or something else happens they would still make a secure income because their clients would have signed a contract for a specific amount of time. While this is a positive for them, it isn’t for you. If something does happen in the world of cryptocurrency and the price suddenly drops immediately, you don’t have the choice to opt out of the contract. You will have to continue paying even though you would be making a loss.
To get a better understanding of cloud mining, let’s talk about the advantages of this system. Cloud mining allows you to be completely separate from the operation. You don’t have to worry about where you will put the mining rig or have to deal with the noise. Most of the time you are also not going to be responsible for the maintenance and the repairs that are needed. This is subject to the company you go with, so it is always good to read the fine print of your contract with them. Another advantage of cloud mining is that you really don’t have to know anything about cryptocurrency to get involved. The process happens completely independent of you and any profits just come straight to you. Many people find this part of it very attractive since they want the benefits of mining, but really don’t have the time or knowledge to do it.
With the advantages come the disadvantages. If you want to get into cloud mining it is important that you know exactly what you are getting yourself into from all perspectives. With cloud mining, you will be required to pay upfront and sign a contract for a specific amount of time. There isn’t much flexibility in this, which means that you will be taking all the risk and the company will still get a profit if the price of Bitcoin drops. You will just have to wait it out until the price lifts back up and you never know how long that would be. The other thing you would need to consider is that you have no control over the mining machines. This means that your rewards for mining Bitcoin will be determined on the strength of the equipment of the company. You can’t upgrade it for better equipment that might help you increase your profits. This can be quite limiting. You should also note that cloud mining companies are much bigger targets for hackers. If the system is hacked, all the Bitcoin can be transferred out and then there is nothing you can do about it. It will probably stipulate in the contract the company is not liable to you if this happens (BitDegree, 2021a).
If you are going to go for cloud mining, it is important to pick a company that is reputable. If you know someone who is already in a cloud mining company, and you can personally see that it is working for them and the company is reliable, you can go for it. There are risks with all kinds of Bitcoin mining. Cryptocurrency is naturally volatile and it is difficult to predict what is going to happen in a few months’ time. The price of Bitcoin more than doubled in the space of four months, but it can also drop suddenly. However you choose to mine, you should always be aware of the risks versus the reward and make your decision from there.
The Earning Potential
As you can see, this is not exactly the easiest task, so in order for people to be motivated enough to mine there has to be some sort of compensation. This compensation can be quite substantial if you are lucky enough to solve the problem first. Bitcoin miners get paid in Bitcoin. As the price of Bitcoin against the dollar increases so does the interest in mining it.
As of April 2021, one Bitcoin is worth $62,926. That is a hefty price for just one Bitcoin. There was definitely an upward surge in the price of Bitcoin due to Tesla investing $1.5 billion into it. In December 2020 Bitcoin was sitting at just under $30,000 per Bitcoin. That means that the cryptocurrency has more than doubled in value in four months (Best, 2021).
If you successfully mine the Bitcoin, you won’t just get paid one Bitcoin. The amount of Bitcoin you get paid out halves every four years. When Bitcoin was first launched back in 2009, you would get paid 50 Bitcoins for a successful mining endeavor. In 2012, this was halved to 25 Bitcoins. Four years later it was 12.5, and since May of 2020, it stands at 6.25 Bitcoins for mining successfully (Hong, 2021). If you work that out, it means that if someone was to mine Bitcoin and get it right at this point they would earn $393,287.50. That’s no small chunk of change.
Something else to keep in mind about Bitcoin mining is that as time goes on it gets harder and harder to mine it. The mining process has slowed down significantly since when it was first introduced in 2009. The difficulty of mining increases after every 2,016 blocks have been mined. This means that the chance of successfully mining Bitcoin is lessening. If you started mining in 2010, you would probably have quite a healthy stash of Bitcoin. It is still worth it to mine Bitcoin. As you can see, you can earn a really good sum of money from it, but there is no guarantee that you will be successful in it.
Risks Involved With Mining Bitcoin
As with everything that is worth doing, there are risks involved. When you invest your money into anything, you should always do it in a calculated way to minimize the risks. However, even when you do this there is always some sort of risk present. Knowing what the risks are helps you to prepare for them.
With Bitcoin mining there are two types of risks present: A financial risk and a regulatory risk. The financial risk is where you could spend a bunch of money buying the equipment for Bitcoin mining and then no see no return on investment. This risk can be mitigated through the use of a mining pool. When you are investing there is no guarantee that you will get back more than you put in. This is why it is so important to understand what you are doing before you jump in. Novice Bitcoin miners should always start investing with a mining pool until they have gotten more experience with it. After you have gotten enough experience, you can decide to move onto solo mining. Most people stick to mining in pools because it is a more stable source of income.
The regulatory risks are based on the country you are from. Some countries are very strict with Bitcoin mining regulations. If you are not sure where your country stands on this matter you should do some research and find out. It would be very disappointing to invest in an ASIC mining rig only to find out that you actually aren’t able to do it in your country. The other risk is that countries can change their regulations at any time which can make it difficult if you are already a miner. Cryptocurrency has not been around long enough for anyone to perfectly predict what will happen in the future. If a government sees some sort of potential risk or negative aspect, they could restrict certain things pertaining to investing and mining in Bitcoin. If you live in a country that is already uncertain about where they stand with Bitcoin, then the risks are definitely higher.
Another risk with mining Bitcoin is the fact that it is getting harder and harder to mine it. As this happens more powerful machines will need to be used in order to mine. When Bitcoin first launched, you could mine it with your regular at-home laptop and have no problem. These days that is not possible and the amount of processing power it will take to mine will only get more and more. This means that it is going to take way more energy to mine than it does now. It also means that miners will have to upgrade their mining rigs to make sure that they can continue mining their Bitcoin. Right now it looks like the growth of the network is going to go much further ahead of the growth of the technology needed to mine it (Hong, 2021).
There have been concerns on the impact on the environment so much power is needed to mine for Bitcoin. This could increase the chances of governments putting stricter regulations on mining Bitcoin since the carbon footprint of mining for Bitcoin is increasing. At the moment there are efforts to find better and cleaner ways to provide energy to the mining operations. Other cryptocurrencies are also working on solutions for this. One of those is Ethereum, which we will touch on later on in the book.
If you are looking to invest in Bitcoin mining it can definitely be lucrative, but there are risks involved. If you are able to put down the money to invest in it then by all means you should go for it. A majority of the people who join mining pools get return on investment. As you continue to mine make sure that you stay on top of what is happening in the Bitcoin and cryptocurrency world. It is important to be knowledgeable in the areas you are placing your investments in.

Chapter 7:
How to Buy Bitcoin and Other Cryptocurrency
Many people want to buy Bitcoin and other cryptocurrency, but they just don’t know how. It works better when you know the steps and can go from there. Thankfully it is much easier now than it was when cryptocurrency was first released and introduced to the world. Let’s go through the process of buying Bitcoin should you want to do so.
As it stands right now Bitcoin has the highest hash rate and security of all the cryptocurrencies. This means that it has won the store value front. You can buy more with Bitcoin than any other cryptocurrency out there. In the future this might change, but that doesn’t look like it will happen in the immediate future. Right now the other cryptocurrencies are battling it out for smart contracts. It will be interesting to see how this plays out in the future.
Steps to Buy Cryptocurrency
There are many ways to purchase cryptocurrencies. As it rises in popularity it is actually getting easier to do so. This is great news for those that are very serious about buying cryptocurrency. If you have never bought Bitcoin or other cryptocurrencies, then you can find out the many different ways you can get into it below.
Sign Up for an Exchange Platform
You will need to sign up for a cryptocurrency exchange so that you will be able to buy, sell, and have Bitcoin and any other cryptocurrency. There are many different exchanges out there, but it is best to pick one that will allow you to deposit your Bitcoin into your own personal wallet. It is generally safer to have your Bitcoin separate from the exchange platform, because you don’t have to trust the platform to keep your money.
Coinbase, Kraken, Gemini, and Binance U.S. are the most common exchange platforms. there are plenty more so you can do your own research to find out what your options are. When you are setting up your account, make sure that you are keeping safe. These platforms are still on the internet and although Bitcoin has very little risk of being hacked, these platforms can be. There is a risk of being hacked or having your Bitcoins stolen because many exchange programs are still centralized and therefore do not have the same safety protocols as Bitcoin. Use a two-factor authentication and make sure your password is long and unique. This will make it harder for people to get into your account. As long as you are safe and keep your Bitcoin in your own wallet, your cryptocurrency will be secure (Conway, 2020b).
Connect Your Payment Options
You will want to link up a bank account to your exchange. The reason for this is that you will need to deposit or withdraw money from your exchange. If you want to buy more Bitcoin or cash out the ones that you have then this is necessary. You should make sure that the bank you are with allows this type of transaction. Some are quite weary of crypto withdrawals. They might stop or put these transactions on hold which is very inconvenient for the user. Most banks do allow these kinds of transactions, but it is always better to check and make sure so that you can avoid any unnecessary issues down the line.
You can link either your bank account, debit or credit card to the exchange. However, it is better to not link the credit card due to the volatility of Bitcoin. You should also be aware that each option is tied to a different fee at the bank. Usually deposition into a bank account has a much higher fee than into your debit or credit card. Do your research so that you aren’t hit with any unexpected fees when the time comes to transact with your cryptocurrency.
Buy Your Cryptocurrency
If you have ever traded in the stock market you will probably be able to navigate the cryptocurrency exchange. These exchanges have the most of the same features as stock brokerage. When you order your cryptocurrency, you can choose options like stop-loss, limit, and market orders. The type of exchange you use will determine what function you have. This is why you need to do your research to make sure you have picked the exchange that will work best for you and your needs.
Store Your Cryptocurrency Safely
Once you have your Bitcoin or other cryptocurrency you should move it out of your exchange and into your wallet. An exchange has a risk of being hacked and your crypto being stolen. Wallets are a much safer place to store your cryptocurrency. Even if your exchange offers you a wallet option it is best not to take it. Wallets are not their primary concern so there is a risk involved here.
We have already mentioned the types of wallets available,, but now we will talk about hot and cold wallets. Both have their place, but you need to be sure that you are using them properly to prevent currency loss. A hot wallet is one that is run on the internet. You have them on devices that can connect to the internet. Because of this, hot wallets can more easily be hacked and your keys stolen. Hot wallets are very convenient because of the ease of transactions in the wallet. Since it is connected to the internet, you will be able to make transactions without much effort from your side. You need to make sure that you are using enough security when you are using a hot wallet.
A cold wallet is one that is not connected to the internet. This adds a level of safety to the storage of your cryptocurrency. It is much more difficult to hack something that is not connected to the internet than something that is. There is a little to no risk of losing your crypto if you keep it in a cold wallet. Users can store their private keys in here and it will stay secure.
You can use hardware wallets or paper wallets. The most secure of the two is a paper wallet. These are used for long term investments as you can’t quickly gain access to it to make a change to your investments. A paper wallet can be generated on certain websites. You can then print your private and public keys and stash it away. If someone finds this paper then they will have all of your information so it is not good to keep this paper on your person. Many people laminate this paper and then keep it in a safe or in a safety deposit box at a bank. It may seem unnecessary, but this is by far the safest way to store your cryptocurrency. If you are someone who has a lot of your finances invested into cryptoassests then this is almost a necessity. Hardware wallets are usually USB devices that your information can be stored on. They are not connected to the internet and once you are done using it you can just remove it from the computer. It is also important to keep a hardware wallet safe.
You should have an exchange, cold wallet, and a hot wallet. This will allow you to trade and keep your assets safe. Using each one in the right way will give you the most benefit and security. You should have your exchange ready to buy and sell. Then your hot wallet can be used to store small amounts of cryptocurrency that you wish to trade with, it is available to you wherever you need it. Your cold wallet should then be used to store larger amounts of cryptocurrency that you are holding for the long term (Conway, 2020b).
Cryptocurrency ATMs
The above way is the most popular way to buy and invest your cryptocurrency. It is probably the most convenient way as well, but it is not the only way. You can purchase Bitcoin and most other cryptocurrencies by using a cryptocurrency ATM. You feed your money into the ATM and you can get cryptocurrency in exchange. The cryptocurrency is then transferred to your digital wallet.
Although these ATMs are gaining popularity, you still don’t find them everywhere. If you live in a city or town that does not have one nearby this will probably not be a good option for you, unless you are willing to drive out to find one. There are crypto ATM locators online where you can find the closest ATM to your current location. This will help you determine whether or not the ATM is something that is a good option for you to use on a consistent basis.
Peer-to-Peer Exchanges
With this you will be buying Bitcoin or any other cryptocurrency from someone. You will give them the money in exchange for the cryptocurrency. The person selling it can decide how much they want to sell it for and then you can decide whether you are willing to pay that price or not. This type of exchange is not as anonymous as the decentralized exchanges because it is not using the same system. This is more like a marketplace where you sell your goods and wait for other people to buy them.
You will have to find an exchange service that offers this so that you can have this option. You will be able to set up an account and post when you want to buy or sell crypto. You and the other users will then browse through all the listings and choose which ones they want to contact. From here you can trade using the payment methods, price, and details that are agreed upon.
This method does not offer the same anonymity as regular decentralized Bitcoin and crypto exchanges, but it does have its perks. It offers you the ability to shop around to get the best price whether you are selling or buying. This is helpful if you want to buy crypto, but are not willing to pay the regular price for it. There are various reasons people will be selling their cryptocurrency and these exchange platforms can help you get in contact with those that offer the best deals for you. Many people often prefer this way to sell their cryptocurrency because they get to decide what the price should be, instead of just relying on the market exchange rates.
Broker Exchange
This is just getting in contact with a broker and them handling the process for you. This is definitely the simplest way to do things, but it does man that you will have to disclose your identity. Brokers will need to know your name, address, contact details, and need identification. It will also cost a fee to work with them. This is usually a percentage fee based on a few aspects that the broker will have to disclose to you (BitDegree, 2020).
If you are going with a broker make sure to choose a reputable one. Do your research and ask the broker a lot of questions when you are on the phone with them. They are handling your money so you need to be sure that they can do their job well.
Things to Note When You Are Investing in Cryptocurrency
There has definitely been a rise in people being interested in the world of crypto. It is definitely an interesting topic and something that many people are looking to get involved in. Understanding a few vital things about this industry will help and potential investors go in with the right mindset.
It Is Volatile
Cryptocurrency is quite volatile. You will see it go up and down many times and often in a very short space of time. It is something that can unnerve people who have never experienced it before. Every person who gets involved with crypto must accept that this is how it is and then be prepared for it. There have been a lot of investors that have made emotional decisions and have regretted it. Doing your research and being prepared for the unexpected is of paramount importance in the world of cryptocurrency (Ervin, 2018).
Your Exchange and Wallet Is Not the Same Thing
When first starting out with cryptocurrency people often just have the exchange and don’t bother with setting up a wallet. This is because it is genuinely easier to have an exchange or even a hot wallet and leave it at that. We have already spoken about the importance of having a cold wallet in the previous section. Before you start thinking about getting into cryptocurrency make sure that you go over that section again as there are many people who have lost funds for this simple reason.
Once you understand the need and the purpose of the exchange, hot, and cold wallets it is easily applied. It will save the newbie investor a lot of potential heartache. It is always better to be too prepared than to let things fall through the cracks, especially when it comes to monetary topics.
Use a Different Email Address
One of the biggest draws to cryptocurrency is the anonymity that it gives. If you want to stay anonymous and prevent being attacked by hackers, having a separate email account dedicated to your cryptocurrency trading. You can pick an email address that is completely different from your name and anything that can be linked back to you. It definitely adds an element of safety.
Along with this you can choose a password that is long and does not relate to anything about you. Having a two-factor authentication system in place for all accounts you will be using to deal with cryptocurrency is a must as this will create more security on that font. It may seem like a lot of extra work, but most people who have been in crypto for a long time agree that this is the way to go and it is better to do this from the jump than to have to change everything once you have already entered the crypto world.

Chapter 8:
Proof of Work Versus Proof of Stake
Proof of Work (PoW) and Proof of Stake (PoS) are just the current mechanisms that are put in place for transactions to take place in a blockchain system. There is an argument going around in the blockchain world about which one is better. Different platforms have adopted the one that they feel works best for them. Some are switching over since they feel that it will be the most beneficial to their system (BitDegree, 2021b).
In order to make your own assumption of which one is better, you would need a full understanding of what each one is. Proof of Stake is definitely gaining in popularity over Proof of Work. Understanding the mechanism and the way it works in the real world is what will bring all of this into perspective and also help you understand the options that are available with the blockchain network.
Both of these mechanisms can only be used in the mining process. Blockchain uses mining as a medium to approve transactions so that there is an incentive to approve the transactions. The way the miner is compensated and the work that goes into the mining process is defined by which mechanism is being used.
Proof of Work
The first ever system that was used to mine and confirm transactions was the Proof of Work (PoW) System. This was Satoshi Nakamoto’s solution to allowing the transactions to go through without the interference of a third party. PoW allows the blockchain to reach a consensus, which means that it is sure that the transaction is valid and there is nothing underhanded going on. This prevents fraud like double spending. This is all based on a type of mathematics called cryptography, which is why this type of currency is known as cryptocurrency (BitDegree, 2021b).
There is quite a lot of effort that is needed in order to mine and to approve transactions using this system. Earlier on we spoke about Bitcoin mining, that is essentially the PoW system in process. The mathematical equations that have to be solved are so difficult that only specific computers can actually solve them. Since the equations can never be repeated, once it has been solved the system knows that the transaction is legit and it can be approved and processed.
Since PoW was the only blockchain system at the time, most of the newer cryptocurrencies took it on. It is a great system that minimizes fraud and other unwanted activity, but it does have its downsides. As you would know by now, it takes a whole lot of resources in order to mine using this system. Anyone who wants to mine would need the right equipment and the electrical power to allow it to run. This has proven to have a negative impact on the environment because of all the electricity used to power the mechanisms. These computers are often continuously running and that really does pull massive amounts of energy per day.
Let’s dive a little deeper into the Proof of Work system. In order for PoW to actually function, it needs the network to prove that there has been work done. This work is the computational power being used. The more work being done, the more likely the person is to mine successfully. This adds incentive for people to mine and use the PoW system. The more miners there are the more transactions can be processed at a faster rate. Bitcoin uses the SHA-256 hashing algorithm in their PoW system. This is the standard one that most other cryptocurrencies also use or at least have used at the beginning of their development (Frankenfield, 2021b).
All the computers will begin hashing so that they can solve the mathematical equation first. Mining is a very competitive process, since you are going up against every other miner to solve that mathematical equation. While it is true that the faster your computer, the more likely you are to mine successfully and solve the equation, mining is more of a random lottery than a race. There is no set finish line and the computer that is successful can really be any one. It is determined purely by luck. If you think of a lottery, the people who buy more tickets will have a higher chance of winning, but it is anyone’s game and there is nothing you can do to secure the win. With mining it is the higher your hash rate or the more computers you have working for you, the higher your chance of being successful, even though there is no way to guarantee it. This is why so many people choose to mine in a pool, so that they can have a higher chance of getting something out.
Since the person that comes out being victorious in the end is randomly chosen in proportion to the work done, it adds an incentive for all the people working on the system to act honestly and make sure that the transactions are true. There needed to be a system in place so that a decentralized network would be possible and that would deter people from trying to cheat the system. With PoW it makes it too resource intensive to try and overtake the system and do something underhanded. There is a lot of work that needs to be done when a system uses PoW, but this makes it more secure than many other ways of doing it (Frankenfield, 2021b).
While PoW is a very secure system and has been working well so far, there are some drawbacks to it. This is why some other cryptocurrencies have decided to move onto another system of verifying transactions. This does not mean that PoW is unable to function or is bad as a system. However, it is good to be aware of the limitations that are involved with this kind of system. This will allow you to understand why there is a move to other systems and perhaps see the solutions to these issues that are facing PoW.
The best way to explain this is by using Bitcoin as an example since the system is the first one to use Proof of Work and show no signs of wanting to switch over to another system. The Bitcoin system takes about 10 minutes to approve a transaction. The Bitcoin blockchain can only handle seven transactions per second. If there are more than this it creates a backlog in the system and the fees start to increase. When Bitcoin first started the transaction fees were a fraction of a cent.
