We have already briefly mentioned interoperability between different chains. This is something has been proposed and followed through by Polkadot. It will allow all blockchains to be connected so that there is no need for central exchange. It will also allow users to have more flexibility with their cryptocurrency and the blockchain networks they are a part of. The truth is that there is no one platform that can do it all, and not having something to connect them all is causing a ceiling for the future of blockchain.
The number of blockchain projects and platforms are on the rise because this technology has sparked so many new and innovative ideas. Having a way to connect all of them seems to be the next logical step in the process of blockchain. Each blockchain handles data differently and has different strengths than the next one. Users will have preferences and it will be very difficult for them to be flexible and connect with the platforms they think would work for them if they are unable to simultaneously connect with the platforms of their choice.
As blockchains are developing, it is becoming clearer that certain platforms are being tailored to specific groups of people. These can be religious groups, labor unions, government departments, and other organizations. This is a natural process since different groups will have different needs and preferences. Having a way to connect them to bigger blockchain networks will allow them to still interact with the big picture while still being able to operate in the preferences and have their needs being catered to. It is very difficult to have one platform that can properly cater to everyone’s needs and preferences. People are complex and there is no one size fits all solution. Having just one system would mean that no matter what it does there will always be some group that is unhappy with something because it does not suit them. Connecting multiple blockchain networks helps to solve this problem.
Blockchain interoperability is cross-chain technology. It will allow information, currency, and any other data to be shared between different chains and blockchain platforms. This innovation will allow people to enjoy the full benefit of the blockchain ledger technology. This lays the foundation for a widespread adoption of blockchain platforms and technology in general. The issue of scalability is also solved here because there will be so many different chains that can all communicate with each other. If we are looking to connect the whole world through technology, this will be the way to do it.
This is all still in the testing phase. There is no doubt there will be some hiccups that will need to be dealt with as this begins to go public. Since it is such a big project there is no way that it will just be able to work on the first try. There will also have to be conversations with all the other blockchain networks to see how this can happen in the most viable and successful way for everyone. All the platforms that do want to be connected will get the opportunity to do so, but there might be some that want to stay separate. This might not be the smartest choice, but if the majority of the token holders in that platform make the detection then there is not much anyone can do about that. This is again the beauty of this blockchain technology; everyone gets to decide what they want to do and won’t be forced into something they do not agree with by a central power.
An innovation that will come along with the connecting of various chains is having a multitoken wallet and facilitating multitoken transactions. As it stands, a person needs different wallets for each cryptocurrency they own and can only transact with one at a time to platforms that will allow it. Having a system in place that will allow the user to have everything in one place and transact with the tokens they have, would make the process a lot easier for the user. They would not need to have multiple wallets to keep track off, this can definitely be a tiresome task. They also would not have to always be converting their tokens into the ones that are needed for the transactions, the system would do it for them. It just creates a more seamless operation as a whole.
Polkadot is not the only one that has an interoperability project on the go. There are many others that have noticed a need for this and jumped on the developing bandwagon to find a way to get it done. Polkadot has gotten the most attention for it because they are the biggest platform that has brought the idea forward. They have also laid out a plan for this to be implemented at some point in the future. The race to interoperability has starred with many other platforms and companies joining in to develop something that is going to suit the needs of blockchain networks and be a good fit for the future of the technology. This will also accelerate the process. The more individuals that are working on the same solution, the quicker it will get done. The other projects that are at work with the interoperability of blockchains are Blocknet, Aion Online, Wanchain, and Cosmos Blockchain. There will definitely be more popping up and there might be some that we are not yet aware of. One thing is for sure, if this is able to be implemented worldwide, it will change the way we do things in a drastic way for the better (Geroni, 2020).

Conclusion
There has been a lot of information given to you in this book. That is because there is a lot of information on the subject of cryptocurrency and blockchain. There is always something more to learn here because it is always changing and developing. It is a good idea to stay abreast with what is going on in the crypto world, so that when things change, you are aware of it. Things happen so fast so if you are out of the loop for a while, you could come back and find that everything is different.
With that being said, the basic principles will always be the same. Once you understand it, it is easier to build on that knowledge. Even if things change they will not be able to change in a way that will make the previous systems and knowledge obsolete for a long time. This is why it is important to have a base knowledge on what the systems can do and what their function is. This book could just be a start in your cryptocurrency journey.
While you don’t need in-depth knowledge in the systems and processes that have built the blockchain networks in order to invest in cryptocurrency, you should know what you are investing in should you choose to do so. In the future cryptocurrency aims to not only be something to invest in but a system that will allow you to trade and transact in your everyday life. The goal, especially of Bitcoin, was to create an alternate to the money that we use today. It was designed to give back control to the end user, instead of everything being controlled by a central entity that had the ultimate power over how money works and how people use it.
Cryptocurrency isn’t just a fad that will pass in a few years. It is something that has been built to support the way we want to do life. It is no secret that there are some serious limitations in the way we handle our finances now. These limitations will put a cap on how we can grow as a society. The mark of a healthy society is one that keeps on growing and changing. Once something stops growing it becomes stagnant and that is just a short way from degradation. We should see these moves towards a new way of doing things as something healthy and a mark that we are progressing as a society.
The future of cryptocurrency is still not completely set in stone. There is no way that the current financial system will be wiped out and replaced with cryptocurrency any time soon. There is a lot that would need to be considered and it would also depend on what the rules and regulations of the countries that would take it on. There are still many countries that are not that excited about this idea and there are some strict laws in place regarding all kinds of cryptocurrency. This means that there is still a lot of work to be done before there is a hope to have this as a universal currency. Perhaps one day it will be possible to have a currency system that connects the world together. This will definitely make it easier to trade overseas and become a more global economy.
Even though we are not there yet, cryptocurrency has really come a long way since it really started. Bitcoin only launched in 2011, which wasn’t so long ago. If you look at how it has taken off and how many people have bought into the idea in such a short amount of time, it is really promising. This is a space that is definitely worth watching because you never know when the next big innovation will happen and that could be the thing that changes the way we do things forever.
The aspect of cryptocurrency that has drawn the most attention is the blockchain technology and the fact that it can operate in a decentralized manner. This is a technology that can be used for so much more than just financial services. Many companies have caught onto that and are doing their best to capitalize on it. The possibilities are endless when it comes to the use of blockchain. Ethereum was the first to decide that they wanted to use blockchain for more than just finances. You can create systems, apps, and platforms that are completely decentralized. This will allow the user to be more in control of everything that happens with regards to their information and anything else they put onto the system. After all the stories of companies being hacked or selling the users information this is definitely a welcome innovation. The future is definitely something to get excited about when it comes to this type of technology. There has been nothing quite as innovative in a long time. It is no wonder that it has created such a buzz in almost all circles.
This book was not designed to give any financial advice or advise about this type of technology in general. It was designed to give you, the reader, a good look at what cryptocurrency really is about and hopefully spark some interest in the future potential of it. By now, you should have a good idea of what cryptocurrency is and what blockchain technology is capable of. It is definitely a complex topic that has lots of different parts that come together to form a working system. If you are able to wrap your head around what was said in this book you will be able to go into the world with a working knowledge of all the topics covered. Having the big picture and knowing all the intricacies of what makes this system work is something that will be so important for the future.
While this is definitely something that is fun to know about, it doesn’t just stop there. Understanding these topics will be integral to keeping up with what is happening with technology. There is no doubt in my mind that blockchain and cryptocurrency will be around for a long time. Not only that, but it will be integrated into many other parts of society in order to better the processes and systems that we already have in place. This has already started happening in many different avenues in the corporate world. Sectors ranging all the way from healthcare to supply chain are looking and have found ways to integrate blockchain into the way they do things. Walmart, Ford, Pfizer, and British Airways are just some of the companies that have integrated blockchain into some part of their company. They may be ahead of the game but there is no doubt that many other companies and groups will be following suit. This is definitely something big that should be celebrated and understanding the systems and having a good knowledge base to draw from is the start to being a part of the exciting time ahead for cryptocurrency and blockchain as a whole.
